## 选一本适合讨论双重视角的小说 · p06-a-01

主题：文娱与运动；情景：选择读书小组作品（reading-group-book-choice）。

沟通目标：比较故事题材与讨论安排后选定共读作品

背景：读书小组成员玛拉和伊沃正在选择下个月的共读作品；书名及情节均为虚构。

人物：Mara、Ivo。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-01-s01 · Mara: Do you have a recommendation for our next reading-group book?
- p06-a-01-s02 · Ivo: The Night Ferry. Its narrative moves between a mother and her adult son.
- p06-a-01-s03 · Mara: Is the plot hard to follow?
- p06-a-01-s04 · Ivo: Not really. It's about their search for a missing letter, rather than a complicated mystery.
- p06-a-01-s05 · Mara: Personally, I'd prefer that to another crime novel. Is it a long book?
- p06-a-01-s06 · Ivo: Only 180 pages. I found the ending powerful, especially when the son finally reads the letter.
- p06-a-01-s07 · Mara: Then let's read that. We can discuss the two viewpoints at our meeting on the last Thursday.
- p06-a-01-s08 · Ivo: Agreed. I'll put the title and date in our group message.

目标用法：

- recommendation · n. · 推荐的作品；义项 sense-staged-ox-73455551ee08fdec-p06-a-01；来源 ox-73455551ee08fdec；证据 p06-a-01-s01；词形 recommendation。
- narrative · n. · 故事的叙述；义项 sense-staged-ox-7c600a1faf3adb83-p06-a-01；来源 ox-7c600a1faf3adb83；证据 p06-a-01-s02；词形 narrative。
- plot · n. · 故事情节；义项 sense-staged-ox-1ab6257761a701ac-p06-a-01；来源 ox-1ab6257761a701ac；证据 p06-a-01-s03；词形 plot。
- personally · adv. · 就个人而言；义项 sense-staged-ox-ce58dd44effb44d2-p06-a-01；来源 ox-ce58dd44effb44d2；证据 p06-a-01-s05；词形 personally。
- powerful · adj. · 有强烈感染力的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7838a3c375425f0d-p06-a-01；来源 ox-7838a3c375425f0d；证据 p06-a-01-s06；词形 powerful。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The book request develops into a concrete comparison of viewpoint, plot complexity, length and emotional effect. Mara chooses The Night Ferry and both agree on a last-Thursday discussion and group-message follow-up. The fictional context, two consistent reading-group speakers and leisure classification fit the completed selection.

## 请朋友帮忙缝回纽扣 · p06-a-02

主题：居家生活；情景：请同伴指导缝回纽扣（shirt-button-sewing-help）。

沟通目标：确认纽扣位置与针线工具，并约定先在碎布上练习再处理衬衫

背景：艾达想保留一件旧衬衫，请会缝纫的朋友乔帮忙处理掉落的纽扣。

人物：Ada、Jo。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-02-s01 · Ada: Can you help me sew this button back on? I want to keep the shirt.
- p06-a-02-s02 · Jo: Of course. I like these old-fashioned buttons. Where did this one come off?
- p06-a-02-s03 · Ada: The cuff. The tailor left a pin where the button should go.
- p06-a-02-s04 · Jo: Good, we have the position. I'll remove that and thread a needle.
- p06-a-02-s05 · Ada: My stitches never look neat. Could I watch you do the first few?
- p06-a-02-s06 · Jo: Yes. Then you can try. Don't pull the thread too tight or the button won't sit properly.
- p06-a-02-s07 · Ada: Let's practise on this scrap of cloth first. I don't want to spoil the cuff.
- p06-a-02-s08 · Jo: Good idea. I'll show you here before we touch the shirt.

目标用法：

- old-fashioned · adj. · 老式的；义项 sense-staged-ox-bb971f8bbb5ef0e9-p06-a-02；来源 ox-bb971f8bbb5ef0e9；证据 p06-a-02-s02；词形 old-fashioned。
- pin · n. · 大头针；义项 sense-staged-ox-0f8e09664b90ff06-p06-a-02；来源 ox-0f8e09664b90ff06；证据 p06-a-02-s03；词形 pin。
- needle · n. · 缝纫针；义项 sense-staged-ox-adf2012f1518a100-p06-a-02；来源 ox-adf2012f1518a100；证据 p06-a-02-s04；词形 needle。
- neat · adj. · 整齐的；义项 sense-staged-ox-8f9f333ac26e0871-p06-a-02；来源 ox-8f9f333ac26e0871；证据 p06-a-02-s05；词形 neat。
- properly · adv. · 正确地；合适地；义项 sense-staged-ox-0b4787fecc8f2b20-p06-a-02；来源 ox-0b4787fecc8f2b20；证据 p06-a-02-s06；词形 properly。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Ada’s request to preserve a shirt leads to identifying the cuff position and tools, a sensible tension warning, and an explicit agreement to practise on scrap cloth before touching the shirt. Jo remains the knowledgeable helper; the goal accurately promises preparation and practice, not an already repaired cuff.

- ox-bb971f8bbb5ef0e9：accepted；In s02, "old-fashioned" directly modifies "buttons" and expresses their traditional style; Jo’s liking them makes the use natural rather than derogatory.（p06-a-02-s02）。
- ox-0f8e09664b90ff06：accepted；In s03, "a pin" is the count-noun object left at the button position; removing it before threading a needle distinguishes the marking pin from the sewing tool.（p06-a-02-s03）。

## 把采访问题缩小到本地住房计划 · p06-a-03

主题：社会与公共事务；情景：拟定校报地方住房采访问题（student-news-interview-question）。

沟通目标：把宽泛的政治采访题目缩小为住房计划的具体问题，并删去缺乏依据的群体立场表述

背景：在虚构的校报编辑练习中，编辑莉娜和学生记者阿米尔讨论采访一位地方政治人物的问题。

人物：Lina、Amir。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-03-s01 · Lina: Our next interview is with a local politician. What do you want to ask?
- p06-a-03-s02 · Amir: Why is poverty still a problem across the nation?
- p06-a-03-s03 · Lina: That is a huge question. Our school paper mainly covers local politics.
- p06-a-03-s04 · Amir: Then I could ask about the council's housing policy. The official council report says ten new flats are planned.
- p06-a-03-s05 · Lina: Better. Ask who those flats are intended for and when people can apply.
- p06-a-03-s06 · Amir: Can I say that young people support the plan?
- p06-a-03-s07 · Lina: We only spoke to three students. Their views don't represent all young people.
- p06-a-03-s08 · Amir: You're right. I'll remove that claim and keep the questions about the flats.
- p06-a-03-s09 · Lina: And let's put the same questions to the other political groups, so this isn't just publicity for one of them.
- p06-a-03-s10 · Amir: Agreed. I'll draft the housing questions first, then we can check that the wording is fair.

目标用法：

- politician · n. · 政治人物；义项 sense-staged-ox-9f67f37d47bcf8f3-p06-a-03；来源 ox-9f67f37d47bcf8f3；证据 p06-a-03-s01；词形 politician。
- poverty · n. · 贫困；义项 sense-staged-ox-37af7c0fcb314888-p06-a-03；来源 ox-37af7c0fcb314888；证据 p06-a-03-s02；词形 poverty。
- nation · n. · 国家；义项 sense-staged-ox-fff1bfa989af3200-p06-a-03；来源 ox-fff1bfa989af3200；证据 p06-a-03-s02；词形 nation。
- politics · n. · 政治事务；义项 sense-staged-ox-1c73a4880747e2f2-p06-a-03；来源 ox-1c73a4880747e2f2；证据 p06-a-03-s03；词形 politics。
- policy · n. · 政策；义项 sense-staged-ox-ea00a3ec307f3afa-p06-a-03；来源 ox-ea00a3ec307f3afa；证据 p06-a-03-s04；词形 policy。
- official · adj. · 官方的；义项 sense-staged-ox-6617535f8aa42c1a-p06-a-03；来源 ox-6617535f8aa42c1a；证据 p06-a-03-s04；词形 official。
- represent · v. · 代表；义项 sense-staged-ox-1b149a80a218a81c-p06-a-03；来源 ox-1b149a80a218a81c；证据 p06-a-03-s07；词形 represent。
- political · adj. · 政治的；义项 sense-staged-ox-fd1e3a4a4604e48a-p06-a-03；来源 ox-fd1e3a4a4604e48a；证据 p06-a-03-s09；词形 political。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The editor redirects a national poverty question to local housing and catches an unsupported claim based on three interviews. The journalist accepts removal and a concrete housing-question plan, while agreeing to fair coverage across groups. Roles remain stable and the society scenario’s narrowing and correction are both achieved.

## 为能源展示区分预测与证据 · p06-a-04

主题：研究与论证；情景：修订课堂能源展示的证据表述（classroom-energy-claim-revision）。

沟通目标：区分历史数据与预测，承认相关关系不等于因果，并约定修订图表标题和说明

背景：诺亚向同学萨米展示课堂报告草稿；两人讨论的图表和国家均为虚构教学材料。

人物：Noah、Sami。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-04-s01 · Noah: Could you check this slide for my presentation on nuclear energy?
- p06-a-04-s02 · Sami: Your chart shows energy prices for two years. Why does the title say that a new power station will lower bills?
- p06-a-04-s03 · Noah: Prices fell in the year after it opened. I thought that would prove my point.
- p06-a-04-s04 · Sami: Not necessarily. Other things could have changed in the same year.
- p06-a-04-s05 · Noah: So I can describe the relation between the two events, but not say that one caused the other?
- p06-a-04-s06 · Sami: Exactly. And your figure for next year is a prediction, not a recorded price.
- p06-a-04-s07 · Noah: I'll label it clearly and change the title to a question about prices.
- p06-a-04-s08 · Sami: That works. Put the possible alternative explanations underneath the chart too.
- p06-a-04-s09 · Noah: I will. Then the slide will show what we know and what still needs checking.

目标用法：

- presentation · n. · 口头展示；义项 sense-staged-ox-06f64205f5967997-p06-a-04；来源 ox-06f64205f5967997；证据 p06-a-04-s01；词形 presentation。
- nuclear · adj. · 核能的；义项 sense-staged-ox-cb310dae77f87dd3-p06-a-04；来源 ox-cb310dae77f87dd3；证据 p06-a-04-s01；词形 nuclear。
- prove · v. · 证明；义项 sense-staged-ox-16e06f4acf6c408f-p06-a-04；来源 ox-16e06f4acf6c408f；证据 p06-a-04-s03；词形 prove。
- necessarily · adv. · 必然地；义项 sense-staged-ox-ef61b703692d0b9b-p06-a-04；来源 ox-ef61b703692d0b9b；证据 p06-a-04-s04；词形 necessarily。
- relation · n. · 关系；联系；义项 sense-staged-ox-321e467ef92afa86-p06-a-04；来源 ox-321e467ef92afa86；证据 p06-a-04-s05；词形 relation。
- prediction · n. · 预测；义项 sense-staged-ox-80a6662340c64453-p06-a-04；来源 ox-80a6662340c64453；证据 p06-a-04-s06；词形 prediction。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-a；整篇结论：accepted。

A peer checks a slide, challenges a causal claim, separates a future prediction from recorded data and agrees with the author on relabelling and alternative explanations. The final turn commits to those revisions, matching the stated agreement goal. The fictional exercise avoids presenting the causal inference as fact.

- ox-cb310dae77f87dd3：accepted；In s01, "nuclear energy" is an ordinary adjective-noun collocation specifying energy from nuclear processes, consistent with the subsequent power-station discussion.（p06-a-04-s01）。
- ox-16e06f4acf6c408f：accepted；In s03, "would prove my point" is a modal-plus-transitive-verb construction meaning establish the claim. Its proposed use is challenged in s04, not endorsed as sufficient evidence.（p06-a-04-s03）。
- ox-ef61b703692d0b9b：accepted；In s04, "Not necessarily" is an idiomatic adverbial response denying inevitability; the possible other changes explicitly explain why the conclusion is not necessary.（p06-a-04-s04）。

## 选一条安静的观鸟步道 · p06-a-05

主题：自然与环境；情景：选择安静的湖边观鸟路线（guided-bird-walk-route）。

沟通目标：按观鸟兴趣选定步道及停留位置，并确认不离开小径靠近鸟巢

背景：游客埃琳向自然公园向导本咨询短程观鸟路线。鸟种和公园场景为虚构。

人物：Erin、Ben。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-05-s01 · Erin: I'd like to spend an hour outdoors, somewhere away from the playground.
- p06-a-05-s02 · Ben: Try the path along the northern edge of the lake. It is usually peaceful in the morning.
- p06-a-05-s03 · Erin: Are the small brown birds near that path native to this area?
- p06-a-05-s04 · Ben: Yes. They nest here every spring. You might also see a rare visitor, but I would not count on it.
- p06-a-05-s05 · Erin: The ordinary lake birds are enough for me. Can I leave the path to get a closer photograph?
- p06-a-05-s06 · Ben: Please stay on it and use your zoom. The reeds beside it contain nests.
- p06-a-05-s07 · Erin: Understood. I'll take the lake path and watch from a distance.
- p06-a-05-s08 · Ben: Here it is on the map. The bench halfway along gives you a clear view.

- outdoors · adv. · 在户外；义项 sense-staged-ox-2ffe7a62fa082ca0-p06-a-05；来源 ox-2ffe7a62fa082ca0；证据 p06-a-05-s01；词形 outdoors。

- northern · adj. · 北部的；义项 sense-staged-ox-d23eb733d2e47806-p06-a-05；来源 ox-d23eb733d2e47806；证据 p06-a-05-s02；词形 northern。
- peaceful · adj. · 安静的；义项 sense-staged-ox-40f3fb40899db33d-p06-a-05；来源 ox-40f3fb40899db33d；证据 p06-a-05-s02；词形 peaceful。
- native · adj. · 本地原生的；义项 sense-staged-ox-0ac2b0bae86a3f99-p06-a-05；来源 ox-0ac2b0bae86a3f99；证据 p06-a-05-s03；词形 native。
- rare · adj. · 罕见的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5384cf9866a5974d-p06-a-05；来源 ox-5384cf9866a5974d；证据 p06-a-05-s04；词形 rare。

Erin requests a quiet hour, Ben recommends the northern lakeside route, and the bird discussion leads to clear boundaries around nests. Erin accepts the path and distant viewing; Ben supplies a halfway bench as the viewing location. Nature taxonomy, guide/visitor roles and the route-selection ending are coherent.

## 借好初次羽毛球体验的器材 · p06-a-06

主题：文娱与运动；情景：确认初学羽毛球器材与集合点（beginner-badminton-equipment）。

沟通目标：确认可借用的球拍归属，并约定到指定球网旁等待教练

背景：初次参加社区羽毛球体验的凯前来问器材借用，教练鲁思指引他加入初学组。

人物：Kai、Ruth。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-06-s01 · Kai: Is this the queue for the beginner session?

- p06-a-06-s05 · Kai: Thanks. Should we start at the court by the door?
- p06-a-06-s06 · Ruth: Use the court at the far end. Its net is already up. I'll meet you there after you collect the rackets.

## 探视时先找护士再安静陪伴 · p06-a-07

主题：健康与照护；情景：商定术后探视中的帮助方式（post-operation-visitor-help）。

沟通目标：尊重病人的隐私与陪伴需要，约定请护士前来并在谈话后安静陪伴

背景：虚构的医院探视场景中，朋友米拉来看术后的莱昂；对话不提供诊断或治疗建议。

人物：Mira、Leon。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-07-s01 · Mira: How are you feeling after the operation?
- p06-a-07-s02 · Leon: Tired. It's still painful when I move, and I'd rather not talk much.
- p06-a-07-s03 · Mira: You look pale. Shall I ask the nurse to come over?
- p06-a-07-s04 · Leon: Yes, please. I want to tell her how I feel before she gets busy.
- p06-a-07-s05 · Mira: Would you like me to call your family as well?
- p06-a-07-s06 · Leon: Not yet. I'd like this conversation with the nurse to be private.
- p06-a-07-s07 · Mira: Of course. I ought to ask what you need instead of making plans for you.
- p06-a-07-s08 · Leon: Could you remain here afterwards, just for a little while?
- p06-a-07-s09 · Mira: Yes. I'll find the nurse now, step outside while you talk, and come back when you're ready.

- pale · adj. · 脸色苍白的；义项 sense-staged-ox-b0290bad9bef9290-p06-a-07；来源 ox-b0290bad9bef9290；证据 p06-a-07-s03；词形 pale。

- ought · modal v. · 应该；义项 sense-staged-ox-96dbd0c307015b47-p06-a-07；来源 ox-96dbd0c307015b47；证据 p06-a-07-s07；词形 ought。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The visit moves naturally from discomfort to a request for nursing help, then establishes privacy and the patient’s wish for quiet company afterwards. Mira’s final offer covers finding the nurse, stepping outside and returning. No diagnosis or treatment is supplied, and the consent-sensitive plan matches the health scenario.

## 给户外工作坊留好雨天方案 · p06-a-08

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：分工准备户外工作坊雨天方案（outdoor-workshop-rain-plan）。

沟通目标：确认室内备用场地，并约定场地决定时间及搬运和通知分工

背景：社区活动组织者黛安和志愿者奥马尔讨论周六工作坊，尚未发布最终场地通知。

人物：Diane、Omar。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-08-s01 · Diane: I'm the organizer of Saturday's outdoor workshop. Could you help with the rain plan?
- p06-a-08-s02 · Omar: Certainly. How many people want to participate?
- p06-a-08-s03 · Diane: Twelve so far, possibly a few more. The hall can take twenty.
- p06-a-08-s04 · Omar: Then we should be prepared for everyone to move inside. Is the hall definitely available?
- p06-a-08-s05 · Diane: Yes, I have the key. The planning is nearly finished, but I haven't assigned anyone to contact the group.
- p06-a-08-s06 · Omar: I can do that. You are very organized with the equipment, so why don't you handle moving it?
- p06-a-08-s07 · Diane: That works. I'll decide on the venue at eight on Saturday and call you.
- p06-a-08-s08 · Omar: Then I'll send the group the final location. Until that call, I won't tell them that we're moving indoors.

## 核对贺卡与礼物包裹邮资 · p06-a-09

- p06-a-09-s05 · Sophie: I'll pay by card. Do I tap here?
- p06-a-09-s06 · Pat: Yes. Your payment has gone through. Here is your receipt.
- p06-a-09-s07 · Sophie: Thank you. Do you take both items now?

## 分工煮一锅咖喱蔬菜汤 · p06-a-10

主题：饮食沟通；情景：分工煮汤并安排剩余份量（soup-cooking-and-portioning）。

沟通目标：确定汤的配料、调味与口感，分配烹饪工作并约定保存多余份量

背景：室友露西亚和芬恩准备用蔬菜煮汤，两人协调口味、烹饪分工和多余份量。

- p06-a-10-s01 · Lucia: Shall we make soup with these carrots and lentils?
- p06-a-10-s02 · Finn: Yes. I'll soften the onion in this pan while you get the large pot ready.
- p06-a-10-s03 · Lucia: How much curry powder do you want? I like it, but not too much.
- p06-a-10-s04 · Finn: Just a little. I am not particularly keen on very spicy soup either.
- p06-a-10-s05 · Lucia: All right. Is this enough stock, or should I open another carton?
- p06-a-10-s06 · Finn: That's plenty. Pour half into the pot first; we can add the rest if the soup is too thick.
- p06-a-10-s07 · Lucia: Then I'll add the carrots and lentils once the onion is ready. This looks like more than two servings.
- p06-a-10-s08 · Finn: It is. We can eat half tonight and put the extra portions in the fridge for tomorrow.
- p06-a-10-s09 · Lucia: Good. I'll get the containers out while you stir the onion.

## 先试拍一张轻松的肖像照 · p06-a-11

主题：文娱与运动；情景：调整肖像试拍方式以减轻紧张（portrait-session-comfort-plan）。

沟通目标：照顾被拍者的紧张感，商定先边聊天边试拍一张并共同查看

背景：业余摄影师伊内丝为朋友迪恩拍摄个人肖像，迪恩不习惯面对镜头。

人物：Dean、Ines。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-11-s01 · Dean: I need a portrait for my music-club profile, but I always freeze in front of a camera.
- p06-a-11-s02 · Ines: You don't have to stare at the lens. We can start with you sitting by the window.
- p06-a-11-s03 · Dean: Do you work as a photographer now?
- p06-a-11-s04 · Ines: No, photography is still a hobby. I have a passion for it, though, especially pictures of people I know.
- p06-a-11-s05 · Dean: That makes me feel better. Please tell me if my smile looks forced.
- p06-a-11-s06 · Ines: I will. Just tell me about your last concert and let yourself smile naturally.
- p06-a-11-s07 · Dean: Talking about music might help me feel more relaxed. Can we check the first picture together?
- p06-a-11-s08 · Ines: Absolutely. We'll take one test shot by the window, look at it, and change anything you dislike.
- p06-a-11-s09 · Dean: Great. I'll sit here and tell you about the drummer who forgot his sticks.

## 为读诗晚会选择开场作品和引文 · p06-a-12

主题：文娱与运动；情景：安排读诗晚会开场与节目引文（poetry-reading-selection）。

沟通目标：选定开场诗作，取得作者对短引文的同意，并确认出处信息和朗读分工

背景：社区读诗活动主持人温与受邀诗人费利克斯准备节目。作品《River Windows》是此虚构场景中的诗人原创。

人物：Wen、Felix。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-12-s01 · Wen: You're our guest poet on Thursday. Which piece would you like to read first?
- p06-a-12-s02 · Felix: River Windows. It is a short poem from my new booklet, and it is a pleasure to read aloud.
- p06-a-12-s03 · Wen: That sounds right for a poetry evening with several first-time visitors. Could I quote two lines in the programme?
- p06-a-12-s04 · Felix: Yes, use the opening two lines. They give people the setting without giving away the ending.
- p06-a-12-s05 · Wen: Should the quotation have the title underneath it?
- p06-a-12-s06 · Felix: Please add a full reference: my name, the title, and page four of the booklet.
- p06-a-12-s07 · Wen: I'll do that. I'll introduce you, and then you can read the whole piece.
- p06-a-12-s08 · Felix: Agreed. I'll bring my marked copy so I can begin straight after the introduction.

## 发现花园库房危险物品后报告 · p06-a-13

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：报告社区花园库房的危险物品（garden-shed-hazard-report）。

背景：社区花园志愿者梅和当值负责人休发现开放库房里有不宜让访客接触的物品，决定交由管理人员处理。

人物：Mei、Hugh。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-13-s01 · Mei: I found a tin of rat poison on the floor of the garden shed. I have not touched it.
- p06-a-13-s02 · Hugh: Thanks for telling me. People from the neighbourhood will be here soon, so we need to keep them out of the shed.
- p06-a-13-s03 · Mei: There is also a tray of berries beside it. Are those poisonous?
- p06-a-13-s04 · Hugh: I don't know. Leave both things alone and let the garden manager identify them.
- p06-a-13-s05 · Mei: Does the manager require this kind of product to be locked away?
- p06-a-13-s06 · Hugh: Yes. It needs proper storage, not an open shelf. I'll close the shed and call her now.
- p06-a-13-s07 · Mei: I'll stay at the entrance and direct visitors to the main garden until she arrives.
- p06-a-13-s08 · Hugh: Thank you. Tell them the shed is closed, but do not ask anyone to move the tin.

- p06-a-14-s08 · Vic: I can come between nine and ten. Please leave the cupboard accessible, but do not take anything apart.

## 问清跨信仰活动是否必须参与祈祷 · p06-a-16

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：确认跨信仰开放活动的参与方式（interfaith-visit-participation-check）。

沟通目标：澄清来访者不必参加祈祷，并商定其参加欢迎环节与等候的安排

背景：居民罗丝准备参加当地教堂的跨信仰开放日，向负责接待的丹咨询参与方式。场所安排为虚构。

人物：Rose、Dan。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-16-s01 · Rose: I saw your invitation to the open day. Can I come if I do not follow a religion?
- p06-a-16-s02 · Dan: Of course. It is a social occasion, not a test of anyone's beliefs. The aim is to bring neighbours together.
- p06-a-16-s03 · Rose: The programme includes a prayer. Would I be expected to pray with everyone?
- p06-a-16-s04 · Dan: No. You can listen quietly, or wait in the courtyard until that part is over.
- p06-a-16-s05 · Rose: Thank you. I want to be respectful, but I am not comfortable joining a religious ceremony.
- p06-a-16-s06 · Dan: That is fine. The priest will explain the choices before it begins, and you can ask me anything at the welcome desk.
- p06-a-16-s07 · Rose: Then I'll come for the welcome talk and wait in the courtyard during the prayer.
- p06-a-16-s08 · Dan: You're very welcome. I'll show you where the courtyard entrance is when you arrive.

## 处理课堂冲突前先听取经过 · p06-a-17

主题：学习与技能；情景：就课堂行为处理约定先了解情况（school-behaviour-response-discussion）。

沟通目标：听取家长对全班处罚的担忧，并约定询问相关师生后再决定回应方式

背景：虚构的小学场景中，家长莎拉向校长摩根提出对全班留堂安排的担忧，双方约定先了解情况。

人物：Sara、Morgan。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-17-s01 · Sara: Are you the principal? I would like to discuss what happened in my son's class.
- p06-a-17-s02 · Morgan: Yes. Please sit down and tell me your concern.
- p06-a-17-s03 · Sara: I came to protest against the decision to keep the whole class inside at break. My son says only two children were involved.
- p06-a-17-s04 · Morgan: I have not heard everyone's account yet. What did he tell you?
- p06-a-17-s05 · Sara: He said there was an argument over a ball. He is only in his first year of primary school, so he may have missed some of it.
- p06-a-17-s06 · Morgan: Thank you for explaining that. I do not want to punish children without understanding what happened.
- p06-a-17-s07 · Sara: Could you speak to the teacher before deciding on any punishment?
- p06-a-17-s08 · Morgan: Yes. I will speak to the teacher and the children today. There will be no whole-class penalty while we review it.
- p06-a-17-s09 · Sara: That is what I was asking for. Please let me know what you find out.
- p06-a-17-s10 · Morgan: I will call you after I have heard their accounts.

目标用法：

- principal · n. · 校长；义项 sense-staged-ox-5c46fb62e6d32dd5-p06-a-17；来源 ox-5c46fb62e6d32dd5；证据 p06-a-17-s01；词形 principal。
- protest · v. · 提出抗议；义项 sense-staged-ox-9c57eb6c46b80013-p06-a-17；来源 ox-9c57eb6c46b80013；证据 p06-a-17-s03；词形 protest。
- primary · adj. · 小学教育的；义项 sense-staged-ox-dd0fc5dc8fc38e5a-p06-a-17；来源 ox-dd0fc5dc8fc38e5a；证据 p06-a-17-s05；词形 primary。
- punish · v. · 惩罚；义项 sense-staged-ox-d2d3de28a15a300a-p06-a-17；来源 ox-d2d3de28a15a300a；证据 p06-a-17-s06；词形 punish。
- punishment · n. · 惩罚；义项 sense-staged-ox-48d682dd4a3e6bca-p06-a-17；来源 ox-48d682dd4a3e6bca；证据 p06-a-17-s07；词形 punishment。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The parent objects to a collective penalty but acknowledges incomplete information, and the principal undertakes to hear the teacher and children before deciding. A temporary no-whole-class-penalty commitment and a promised call resolve the immediate concern. The school-role noun principal is clear despite its multiple source candidates, and the exchange does not prejudge the children.

## 区分短片媒体预览和公开发布 · p06-a-18

背景：短片宣传协调员格斯与剪辑师埃拉核对已完成影片的宣传安排，所有项目和时间均为虚构。

人物：Gus、Ella。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-18-s01 · Gus: Can we start telling people about the film now?
- p06-a-18-s02 · Ella: Yes. Production is finished, and the producer has approved this final version.
- p06-a-18-s03 · Gus: Good. The local press have asked for a preview. I can offer them a private screening on Thursday.
- p06-a-18-s04 · Ella: That works, but the public screening is on Friday. We will release the film online the following Monday.
- p06-a-18-s05 · Gus: Then I will put all three dates in the invitation so nobody confuses them.
- p06-a-18-s06 · Ella: How will we promote the public screening? We do not have money for paid adverts.
- p06-a-18-s07 · Gus: With the approved poster on our club page and a short announcement at the library. I will handle both.
- p06-a-18-s08 · Ella: Great. I will prepare the preview link for the journalists. Send me your invitation before it goes out.
- p06-a-18-s09 · Gus: I will. You can check the dates and the link together.

The team distinguishes a Thursday press preview, Friday public screening and following-Monday online release, then assigns no-cost publicity and a preview link with pre-send checking. ## 算清剧场筹款结余再询问坡道报价 · p06-a-19

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：核对剧场筹款结果（theater-fundraiser-results）。

沟通目标：确认可用筹款和下一项空间改善工作

背景：社区剧场志愿者阿莎与财务志愿者托比一起核对虚构筹款活动的账目。

人物：Asha、Toby。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-19-s01 · Asha: We sold four hundred pounds of tickets. How much did the event cost us?
- p06-a-19-s02 · Toby: One hundred and fifty pounds, including thirty for printing and one hundred and twenty for the room.
- p06-a-19-s03 · Asha: What percentage of our costs went on printing?
- p06-a-19-s04 · Toby: Twenty percent. Thirty is one fifth of one hundred and fifty.
- p06-a-19-s05 · Asha: So our profit was two hundred and fifty pounds after all the costs?
- p06-a-19-s06 · Toby: Yes. That is the amount we can put towards improving the entrance.
- p06-a-19-s07 · Asha: The access group asked about a ramp. Can we tell them we will install one?
- p06-a-19-s08 · Toby: Not yet. We need a proper assessment and a price first.
- p06-a-19-s09 · Asha: Then I will ask the building manager to arrange that and explain that we have two hundred and fifty pounds available.
- p06-a-19-s10 · Toby: Good. I will send you the income-and-cost summary to take with you.

目标用法：

- printing · n. · 印刷；义项 sense-staged-ox-13b7fb5549a676bc-p06-a-19；来源 ox-13b7fb5549a676bc；证据 p06-a-19-s02；词形 printing。
- percentage · n. · 百分比；义项 sense-staged-ox-955b054b244f2892-p06-a-19；来源 ox-955b054b244f2892；证据 p06-a-19-s03；词形 percentage。
- profit · n. · 扣除成本后的收益；义项 sense-staged-ox-ca6de1263491574d-p06-a-19；来源 ox-ca6de1263491574d；证据 p06-a-19-s05；词形 profit。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The income and costs reconcile: 400 minus 150 leaves 250, and 30 of 150 is 20 percent. Both volunteers then agree to seek an assessment and price rather than promise an uncosted ramp, with a financial summary to support the enquiry. The opening monetary shorthand is understandable in context, although "four hundred pounds’ worth of tickets" would be a slightly more polished alternative; it is not a substantive defect.

## 先了解口译职业再考虑转行 · p06-a-20

主题：工作与协作；情景：讨论职业方向（career-change-discussion）。

沟通目标：讨论转行念头并确定低风险了解行业的下一步。

背景：成人学员马特向职业顾问齐娅咨询转行口译，谈到的招聘广告和课程只是虚构练习材料。

人物：Matt、Zia。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-20-s01 · Matt: I enjoy languages, but I do not know much about interpreting as a profession. Should I leave my shop job and train full-time?
- p06-a-20-s02 · Zia: You do not need to quit your job just to explore it. What kind of interpreting interests you?
- p06-a-20-s03 · Matt: Community work. This advert asks for qualified interpreters, but I only have a language-course certificate.
- p06-a-20-s04 · Zia: The qualification listed here is a specialist diploma. Your certificate is not the same one.
- p06-a-20-s05 · Matt: Would I qualify for the diploma course with my current certificate?
- p06-a-20-s06 · Zia: We need to ask the course provider. There is a range of entry routes, but the advert does not explain them.
- p06-a-20-s07 · Matt: I would also like to know whether I enjoy the work before paying for a long course.
- p06-a-20-s08 · Zia: A practical introductory workshop could help. This careers guide is a useful resource for finding questions to ask.
- p06-a-20-s09 · Matt: Then I will read the guide and contact the provider about entry requirements and a workshop. I will keep my job for now.
- p06-a-20-s10 · Zia: Good. Bring their reply next time, and we can work out your options from that.

The learner’s impulse to resign is redirected toward learning about interpreting, distinguishing his existing certificate from a specialist diploma, and checking entry requirements with the provider. He explicitly decides to keep his job while reading the guide and asking about a workshop. The fictional-advert context keeps qualification claims bounded and the work/career goal reaches a concrete low-risk next step.

## 重返骑行先参加短程休闲组 · p06-a-21

主题：文娱与运动；情景：商定重返骑行的非竞赛体验（cycling-club-return-plan）。

沟通目标：澄清俱乐部有休闲路线，并约定先参加一次短程活动和在起点会合

背景：阿伦多年后想恢复骑行，向朋友贝丝询问俱乐部是否只有竞赛活动。

人物：Arun、Beth。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-21-s01 · Arun: Does your club only do racing? I would like to ride again, but not against a clock.
- p06-a-21-s02 · Beth: No. Our Sunday group stops for coffee and waits for everyone at each turn.
- p06-a-21-s03 · Arun: I used to cycle regularly. Now I rarely get the bike out, so I am worried I will slow you down.
- p06-a-21-s04 · Beth: Come for the short route first. There is no prize for finishing before anyone else.
- p06-a-21-s05 · Arun: I would be proud of myself just for getting back on the bike. How far is the short route?
- p06-a-21-s06 · Beth: Five miles, with a stop halfway. You can try it once without joining the club.
- p06-a-21-s07 · Arun: That sounds manageable. I will meet the short-route group this Sunday.
- p06-a-21-s08 · Beth: Great. I will wait with you at the start and introduce you to the ride leader.

Arun’s concern about racing and slowing others down is answered with a supportive coffee-stop group, a five-mile short route and a no-membership trial. He agrees to attend Sunday and Beth offers to meet him at the start. This is a concrete re-entry plan, with consistent friend/newcomer roles and a leisure purpose.

## 让短剧结尾的愤怒转为迟疑 · p06-a-22

主题：文娱与运动；情景：修改短剧结尾表演（short-play-ending-rehearsal）。

沟通目标：根据角色动机调整冲突场景和结尾的表演方式

背景：导演娜奥米与演员奥斯卡排练一部虚构短剧，讨论王子发现公主放走囚犯后的反应。

人物：Naomi、Oscar。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-22-s01 · Naomi: Your performance has plenty of energy, but the final scene feels rushed.
- p06-a-22-s02 · Oscar: I thought the prince should be furious when he sees the empty cell.
- p06-a-22-s03 · Naomi: He is, at first. Then the princess tells him that the prisoner saved her life.
- p06-a-22-s04 · Oscar: So he has a reason to listen instead of calling the guards immediately.
- p06-a-22-s05 · Naomi: Exactly. His reaction should change when he hears that news.
- p06-a-22-s06 · Oscar: Should I pretend that I have not noticed her holding the key?
- p06-a-22-s07 · Naomi: No. Look at the key, then at her. Let the audience see that you are deciding whether to believe her.
- p06-a-22-s08 · Oscar: I will lower my hand instead of ringing the bell. Then I can ask her what happened.
- p06-a-22-s09 · Naomi: Good. Let us try it from her entrance and give that decision time to register.
- p06-a-22-s10 · Oscar: Ready. I will begin beside the empty cell.

The actor accepts the blocking and both agree where to restart. Story characters are clearly distinguished from the director and actor, and the revised performance choice satisfies the scenario.

## 让短片开场更贴近人物日常 · p06-a-23

主题：工作与协作；情景：根据反馈调整影片开头（short-film-feedback）。

沟通目标：剪辑者根据试映反馈调整短片开头。

背景：导演奥利夫与剪辑师尼科讨论虚构短片的试映反馈，决定尝试更安静的开场。

人物：Olive、Nico。

起草：author-phase06-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-a-23-s01 · Olive: The test audience liked the main character, but several people found her opening argument too extreme.
- p06-a-23-s02 · Nico: The previous cut began with her missing the bus. Would that be better?
- p06-a-23-s03 · Olive: Perhaps. Previously, I thought a loud argument would grab people immediately.
- p06-a-23-s04 · Nico: But the reality of her situation is quieter. She is worn out by small delays, not angry with everyone she meets.
- p06-a-23-s05 · Olive: I want the opening to reflect that. We could show her arriving late and quietly apologizing.
- p06-a-23-s06 · Nico: Viewers may find it easier to relate to that moment. It gives them a reason to care about her.
- p06-a-23-s07 · Olive: I still like the energy of the argument. I do not want to reject it without trying another version.
- p06-a-23-s08 · Nico: Can I persuade you to watch a cut with the bus scene first? We can compare the two openings side by side.
- p06-a-23-s09 · Olive: Yes. Keep the argument in the saved version and make a new cut starting at the bus stop.
- p06-a-23-s10 · Nico: I will. We can watch both tomorrow before choosing the final opening.

- previous · adj. · 先前的；义项 sense-staged-ox-a2a9c7d1b4be3fa0-p06-a-23；来源 ox-a2a9c7d1b4be3fa0；证据 p06-a-23-s02；词形 previous。

Director and editor discuss audience feedback, contrast the argument with the character’s quieter daily frustration, and agree to preserve the old cut while preparing a bus-stop alternative for comparison tomorrow. The ending establishes a concrete editing decision rather than falsely claiming the new cut is already made. Professional roles, work taxonomy and character motivation remain coherent.

- p06-a-24-s03 · Amy: Are we related to the woman beside him too?

- p06-a-24-s06 · Samir: Then we should correct the caption and keep a photograph of Grandma's note with it.

## 试听后更换间歇失灵的音箱 · p06-a-25

主题：消费与服务；情景：选择故障音箱的替代品（portable-speaker-return-choice）。

背景：顾客帕维尔带着购买凭证退换一台音箱，店员伊玛现场试听同价替代型号。

人物：Pavel、Imogen。

- p06-a-25-s03 · Pavel: No, it works perfectly then. But I bought it to use away from a socket.
- p06-a-25-s04 · Imogen: Obviously, it needs to work on its battery as well. Your receipt is here, so we can arrange an exchange.
- p06-a-25-s05 · Pavel: I need a reliable connection for my dance class. Could we try the other model before I choose?
- p06-a-25-s06 · Imogen: Certainly. It is the same price. I have unplugged it now; connect your phone and play a track.
- p06-a-25-s07 · Pavel: The music is clear. I have walked across the shop, and it has not cut out or crackled.
- p06-a-25-s08 · Imogen: Would you like this model instead, then?
- p06-a-25-s09 · Pavel: Yes, please. This is the one I want.
- p06-a-25-s10 · Imogen: All right. I have recorded the exchange. Here is your replacement and the updated receipt.

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The customer isolates a battery-only crackle, the clerk confirms exchange eligibility and offers a same-price model, and an unplugged walking/listening test addresses portable use before the customer chooses. The clerk then explicitly records the exchange and hands over the replacement and updated receipt. Services classification, speaker roles and the completed replacement goal all align.

- p06-b-04-s06 · Sailing Instructor: Call tomorrow after nine. We'll have the morning forecast and can discuss any lessons that can go ahead.

## 商定同形材料的浮沉观察 · p06-b-08

主题：学习与技能；情景：澄清浮沉实验的预测（classroom-floating-prediction）。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-08-s01 · Luca: My theory is that objects with the same shape will behave similarly in water.
- p06-b-08-s02 · Ms Chen: That's a prediction we can test. Which objects do you want to use?
- p06-b-08-s03 · Luca: These cubes. One is wood and the other is metal, but the similarity in shape should help us compare them.
- p06-b-08-s04 · Ms Chen: Good. Each cube is made from a different substance. Are they the same size, too?
- p06-b-08-s05 · Luca: Yes, and both are solid, not hollow. I think they'll both sink.
- p06-b-08-s06 · Ms Chen: Write that down before we test them. A scientific report needs your observations, even if they disagree with your prediction.
- p06-b-08-s07 · Luca: I'll draw a table with one row for each cube and record what actually happens.
- p06-b-08-s08 · Ms Chen: Do that, then bring the table to the water tray. We'll test these two cubes first.

The student presents a tentative shape-based prediction and the teacher reframes it as testable rather than established science. Equal size, matching shape and non-hollow samples are specified, with material as the comparison variable. They agree a two-row record and a next test at the tray; the dialogue does not claim the potentially false sinking prediction was verified.

## 分工翻译中学家长通知 · p06-b-09

主题：学习与技能；情景：商定学校通知的翻译方式（school-notice-translation-plan）。

沟通目标：确认通知读者和口头摘要需求，约定翻译及核对分工

- p06-b-09-s01 · Priya: This notice explains how pupils choose subjects at secondary school. Could you translate it into Spanish?
- p06-b-09-s02 · Ben: Yes. Do you need the whole document or just the dates?
- p06-b-09-s03 · Priya: The whole document, please. Some parents find written English difficult, even when their spoken English is good.
- p06-b-09-s04 · Ben: I'll prepare a translation. Do you also want something short to read aloud at the meeting?
- p06-b-09-s05 · Priya: That would help. Could you summarize the choices and the deadline in about a minute?
- p06-b-09-s06 · Ben: Sure. I'll send you the full text and a separate summary tomorrow.
- p06-b-09-s07 · Priya: Thanks. I'll check the dates, and then we can ask Elena to check the Spanish before the meeting.

The school colleagues distinguish a full Spanish translation from a short read-aloud summary, establish tomorrow's delivery, and assign date checking plus a later Spanish check. ## 给窗边幼苗留出合适空间 · p06-b-10

主题：居家生活；情景：调整幼苗种植间距（garden-seedling-spacing）。

沟通目标：根据种子包装说明和当前光照确定花盆与幼苗安排

背景：纳迪娅在家育苗，朋友费利克斯和她一起查看种子包装说明，决定花盆位置和间距。

人物：Nadia、Felix。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-10-s01 · Nadia: The packet says one seed per pot, but I put three in this one. Now all three have sprouted.
- p06-b-10-s02 · Felix: There's another empty pot here. Let's follow the spacing instructions rather than crowd them together.
- p06-b-10-s03 · Nadia: The soil is still damp. Should I leave the pots on this windowsill?
- p06-b-10-s04 · Felix: Yes, that's the bright spot the packet recommends. Plants here tend to lean towards the window, so we can watch how these grow.
- p06-b-10-s05 · Nadia: Will they survive outside yet? The nights are still cold.
- p06-b-10-s06 · Felix: The packet says to wait until after the last frost. Let's keep them inside for now.
- p06-b-10-s07 · Nadia: All right. I'll separate the extra seedlings into their own pots and leave enough space between them.

目标用法：

- seed · n. · 种子；义项 sense-staged-ox-4ce272d8f21d1fe8-p06-b-10；来源 ox-4ce272d8f21d1fe8；证据 p06-b-10-s01；词形 seed。
- soil · n. · 土壤；义项 sense-staged-ox-d889a1c27d4509b2-p06-b-10；来源 ox-d889a1c27d4509b2；证据 p06-b-10-s03；词形 soil。
- tend · v. · 往往会；义项 sense-staged-ox-0de36e1d4989db88-p06-b-10；来源 ox-0de36e1d4989db88；证据 p06-b-10-s04；词形 tend。
- survive · v. · 存活；义项 sense-staged-ox-e83b95ddfee28237-p06-b-10；来源 ox-e83b95ddfee28237；证据 p06-b-10-s05；词形 survive。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The dialogue starts with overcrowded seedlings, consults the packet rather than inventing plant-specific care, and settles on separate pots in the bright indoor location until frost risk passes. Only one spare pot is explicitly pointed out, but the ending is a plan to obtain/use individual pots, not a claim that all repotting has already occurred. The title and arrangement goal are supported.

## 约定与工会代表私下谈话 · p06-b-11

主题：工作与协作；情景：安排私下讨论职场不当言行（workplace-private-report-meeting）。

沟通目标：说明对不当言行的担忧，并约定与工会代表私下讨论

背景：虚构职场情景：阿米拉向工会代表加雷思询问如何私下讨论会议中的不当言论，尚未提交正式投诉。

人物：Amira、Gareth。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-11-s01 · Amira: The way I was treated at yesterday's meeting felt unfair. The comments were about my sex, not my work.
- p06-b-11-s02 · Gareth: I'm sorry you had that experience. As your union representative, I can listen and explain the options.
- p06-b-11-s03 · Amira: There were sexual jokes as well. I don't want the details passed around among the staff.
- p06-b-11-s04 · Gareth: I take concerns like this seriously. I won't forward your notes without discussing that with you first.
- p06-b-11-s05 · Amira: I feel strongly about it, but I'm not ready to make a formal complaint today.
- p06-b-11-s06 · Gareth: We can start with a private conversation. Are you free at four?
- p06-b-11-s07 · Amira: Yes. I'll bring my notes, and we can discuss what I want to do next.
- p06-b-11-s08 · Gareth: I'll meet you in the small meeting room at four. We won't submit anything at that meeting unless you decide to.

目标用法：

- unfair · adj. · 不公平的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c40826da7f82ea6f-p06-b-11；来源 ox-c40826da7f82ea6f；证据 p06-b-11-s01；词形 unfair。
- sex · n. · 性别；义项 sense-staged-ox-275ad262a9567c83-p06-b-11；来源 ox-275ad262a9567c83；证据 p06-b-11-s01；词形 sex。
- union · n. · 工会；义项 sense-staged-ox-b92b487ec2caa54b-p06-b-11；来源 ox-b92b487ec2caa54b；证据 p06-b-11-s02；词形 union。
- sexual · adj. · 与性有关的；义项 sense-staged-ox-eb4c33ef3e456f11-p06-b-11；来源 ox-eb4c33ef3e456f11；证据 p06-b-11-s03；词形 sexual。
- staff · n. · 全体员工；义项 sense-staged-ox-d718ff5811dfa06a-p06-b-11；来源 ox-d718ff5811dfa06a；证据 p06-b-11-s03；词形 staff。
- seriously · adv. · 认真地；义项 sense-staged-ox-631ccf8bfdb68b50-p06-b-11；来源 ox-631ccf8bfdb68b50；证据 p06-b-11-s04；词形 seriously。
- strongly · adv. · 强烈地；义项 sense-staged-ox-5b172e323b8cf58d-p06-b-11；来源 ox-5b172e323b8cf58d；证据 p06-b-11-s05；词形 strongly。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional employee raises sex-based comments and sexual jokes while explicitly withholding a formal complaint. The union representative offers a private four-o'clock meeting and discussion before forwarding notes, with the final turn preserving Amira's choice about submission. The sensitive subject is treated seriously without explicit details or unsupported legal promises, and the scheduling goal is actually agreed.

- ox-b92b487ec2caa54b：accepted；s02: your union representative uses union as an attributive noun naming the workers' organization represented by Gareth, not an adjective candidate.（p06-b-11-s02）。
- ox-eb4c33ef3e456f11：accepted；s03: sexual jokes is an adjective-plus-noun phrase for jokes relating to sex, directly motivating the privacy concern.（p06-b-11-s03）。

## 先查档案再说明历史人物身份 · p06-b-12

主题：学习与技能；情景：核对历史人物的展览用语（museum-worker-status-label）。

沟通目标：区分雇佣仆役和被奴役者，决定先查人物记录再写说明

背景：课堂模拟博物馆项目中，两名学生为一幅虚构历史人物画像拟写标签，避免把不同劳动身份混为一谈。

人物：Dorian、Lena。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-12-s01 · Dorian: The note calls him a servant in a royal household. Should our label say he was employed at the palace?
- p06-b-12-s02 · Lena: It doesn't tell us which building he worked in, or whether he was paid. What else does it say?
- p06-b-12-s03 · Dorian: The family made money from overseas trade, including the sale of enslaved people. Does that mean he was a slave?
- p06-b-12-s04 · Lena: Not necessarily. A servant could be employed, while a slave was treated as property and denied freedom. We need evidence about this person.
- p06-b-12-s05 · Dorian: So we don't know the truth about his situation from this note alone.
- p06-b-12-s06 · Lena: Exactly. Let's check the household records in our source pack before we write the label.
- p06-b-12-s07 · Dorian: I'll look for his name in those records. For now, I'll leave the claim about his status out.

目标用法：

- royal · adj. · 王室的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ed25b6947d9a4a77-p06-b-12；来源 ox-ed25b6947d9a4a77；证据 p06-b-12-s01；词形 royal。
- servant · n. · 仆人；义项 sense-staged-ox-e82eda1462e3426d-p06-b-12；来源 ox-e82eda1462e3426d；证据 p06-b-12-s01；词形 servant。
- trade · n. · 贸易；义项 sense-staged-ox-13b5b360b941517f-p06-b-12；来源 ox-13b5b360b941517f；证据 p06-b-12-s03；词形 trade。
- slave · n. · 被当作财产并剥夺自由的奴隶；义项 sense-staged-ox-c662fbcbf3c1d623-p06-b-12；来源 ox-c662fbcbf3c1d623；证据 p06-b-12-s04；词形 slave。
- truth · n. · 真实情况；义项 sense-staged-ox-f8412dfc98135e17-p06-b-12；来源 ox-f8412dfc98135e17；证据 p06-b-12-s05；词形 truth。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The students resist deriving one man's personal status from a household label or the family's trading history. They distinguish possible employment from enslavement in contextual historical terms, then explicitly agree to inspect records and omit an unsupported claim. This achieves the evidence-checking plan, not a falsely resolved identity, and the fictional frame is maintained.

## 分清手工材料并安排标签整理 · p06-b-13

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-13-s01 · Tessa: Is this set of brushes ours, or does it belong to the painting group?
- p06-b-13-s02 · Omar: It's theirs. Our stuff is in the two boxes by the door.

- p06-b-13-s06 · Omar: Put them together in the shallow tray, so we can finish those before opening a new roll.

- ox-b46872617ceec24c：accepted；s03: this string is a material noun for fine cord, and its later gift-tag use clarifies the physical sense.（p06-b-13-s03）。
- ox-2ca17010d07f88fe：accepted；s04: a tin with spare scissors is a count noun for a metal storage container, not the metal substance itself; in there indicates its capacity to hold string too.（p06-b-13-s04）。
- ox-61ca7615873de3dc：accepted；s05: rolls of tape uses the mass noun for adhesive material, with partly used rolls and craft-room storage supplying a clear referent.（p06-b-13-s05）。
- ox-86d497254ed25dfe：accepted；s07: I'll stick a label on each container is a transitive verb followed by the natural attachment location phrase, not the noun for a piece of wood.（p06-b-13-s07）。

## 交接临时接待助理的第一班工作 · p06-b-14

主题：工作与协作；情景：交接新助理首班职责（first-assistant-shift-handover）。

沟通目标：让新来的临时员工了解工作内容和当班支持人员

背景：临时接待助理朱尔斯第一天到社区办公楼值班，主管马科斯介绍工作范围和支援人员。

人物：Jules、Marcos。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-14-s01 · Jules: This is my first shift on reception. What time do I finish?
- p06-b-14-s02 · Marcos: You're here till six. Nessa takes over then, and I'll be in the office upstairs until five.
- p06-b-14-s03 · Jules: Do I just give visitors a badge?
- p06-b-14-s04 · Marcos: Check who they're meeting first. For security, don't let anyone through the inner door unless their host comes to collect them.
- p06-b-14-s05 · Jules: Where are the visitor forms?
- p06-b-14-s06 · Marcos: In this drawer. There's a supply of spare forms in the cupboard behind you.
- p06-b-14-s07 · Jules: And if someone arrives without an appointment?
- p06-b-14-s08 · Marcos: Call me before you issue a badge. After five, Nessa will be in the back office if you need help.
- p06-b-14-s09 · Jules: Got it. I'll check the appointment list first and call if anything is unclear.

目标用法：

- shift · n. · 工作班次；义项 sense-staged-ox-1bd57ef2bbb108f4-p06-b-14；来源 ox-1bd57ef2bbb108f4；证据 p06-b-14-s01；词形 shift。
- till · prep. · 直到某时间；义项 sense-staged-ox-0788cc5bbaaeae86-p06-b-14；来源 ox-0788cc5bbaaeae86；证据 p06-b-14-s02；词形 till。
- security · n. · 安全防护；义项 sense-staged-ox-f419c9bd3ca5c1e1-p06-b-14；来源 ox-f419c9bd3ca5c1e1；证据 p06-b-14-s04；词形 security。
- unless · conj. · 除非；义项 sense-staged-ox-5784facd328fffa8-p06-b-14；来源 ox-5784facd328fffa8；证据 p06-b-14-s04；词形 unless。
- supply · n. · 储备量；义项 sense-staged-ox-c828d7071154bb6b-p06-b-14；来源 ox-c828d7071154bb6b；证据 p06-b-14-s06；词形 supply。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The new receptionist receives an end time, visitor-entry rules, locations of forms and escalation contacts. Nessa being available in the back office after five is compatible with taking over reception at six, closing the supervisor-availability gap. Jules confirms the first action and help route, so the handover goal is achieved without pretending a shift has been worked.

- ox-0788cc5bbaaeae86：accepted；s02: till six takes a time expression rather than a clause, unambiguously selecting the source preposition over the conjunction candidate.（p06-b-14-s02）。
- ox-f419c9bd3ca5c1e1：accepted；s04: For security names protective access control as an abstract noun, directly supported by the host-collection rule for the inner door.（p06-b-14-s04）。
- ox-5784facd328fffa8：accepted；s04: unless their host comes to collect them introduces a finite exception clause to the entry prohibition, correctly conveying 除非.（p06-b-14-s04）。
- ox-c828d7071154bb6b：accepted；s06: a supply of spare forms is a noun phrase meaning a reserve quantity, explicitly located in the cupboard rather than denoting an act of providing.（p06-b-14-s06）。

## 调整反光并试拍产品短片 · p06-b-15

主题：工作与协作；情景：试拍产品短片的灯光与提示（product-film-lighting-rehearsal）。

沟通目标：调整反光和拍摄提示，完成一次试拍并约定检查录音

背景：摄影师梅和同事拉菲在小型摄影棚试拍水瓶短片，开拍前需要处理反光和讲话提示。

人物：Mae、Rafi。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-15-s01 · Mae: Can we keep the studio silent while we record? The microphone is picking up every conversation.
- p06-b-15-s02 · Rafi: I'll close the door. Are we ready to shoot?
- p06-b-15-s03 · Mae: Almost. Don't shine the lamp straight at the bottle; that shiny label is reflecting the light into the camera.
- p06-b-15-s04 · Rafi: How about this angle? I can still read the name on the screen.
- p06-b-15-s05 · Mae: That's better. The script says you turn the bottle once and then put it down.
- p06-b-15-s06 · Rafi: Should I keep talking throughout the shot, even while I turn it?
- p06-b-15-s07 · Mae: Yes. My raised hand will be your signal to begin, and I'll lower it when we have enough.
- p06-b-15-s08 · Rafi: Okay. Let's try one take.
- p06-b-15-s09 · Mae: That's recorded. The label stayed clear, but let's listen to the sound before we move anything.
- p06-b-15-s10 · Rafi: Good idea. I'll leave the bottle and lamp where they are.

目标用法：

- studio · n. · 摄影棚；义项 sense-staged-ox-5484d191de764d4b-p06-b-15；来源 ox-5484d191de764d4b；证据 p06-b-15-s01；词形 studio。
- silent · adj. · 安静无声的；义项 sense-staged-ox-3e432d86b977f875-p06-b-15；来源 ox-3e432d86b977f875；证据 p06-b-15-s01；词形 silent。
- shoot · v. · 拍摄；义项 sense-staged-ox-f5ee7ad0aad1a99c-p06-b-15；来源 ox-f5ee7ad0aad1a99c；证据 p06-b-15-s02；词形 shoot。
- shine · v. · 把光照向；义项 sense-staged-ox-4e658708ee2c40df-p06-b-15；来源 ox-4e658708ee2c40df；证据 p06-b-15-s03；词形 shine。
- shiny · adj. · 有光泽的；义项 sense-staged-ox-0506684780843f44-p06-b-15；来源 ox-0506684780843f44；证据 p06-b-15-s03；词形 shiny。
- script · n. · 拍摄脚本；义项 sense-staged-ox-fe27e01586e17489-p06-b-15；来源 ox-fe27e01586e17489；证据 p06-b-15-s05；词形 script。
- throughout · prep. · 在整个期间；义项 sense-staged-ox-01dfef487202465d-p06-b-15；来源 ox-01dfef487202465d；证据 p06-b-15-s06；词形 throughout。
- signal · n. · 动作提示信号；义项 sense-staged-ox-1c4487f20a3f0d82-p06-b-15；来源 ox-1c4487f20a3f0d82；证据 p06-b-15-s07；词形 signal。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The recording team handles background chatter, changes a reflective label's lighting angle, clarifies the scripted movement and start cue, then confirms a take was recorded. Asking for a silent studio naturally concerns unwanted background noise, not forbidding the performer's planned speech. The final exchange leaves the sound check pending as the goal states, while confirming a clear label.

- ox-3e432d86b977f875：accepted；s01: keep the studio silent is an object-complement adjective requesting absence of background conversations; the following recording instructions distinguish intentional speech.（p06-b-15-s01）。
- ox-f5ee7ad0aad1a99c：accepted；s02: ready to shoot places shoot after the infinitive marker, selecting the filming verb rather than the competing noun a shoot.（p06-b-15-s02）。

## 为社区戏剧安排角色试演 · p06-b-16

主题：文娱与运动；情景：确认角色分配和排练（community-play-casting）。

沟通目标：剧团根据角色要求讨论试演人选及排练时间。

背景：社区剧团的新成员埃利斯担心在陌生人面前表演，导演莫和他讨论适合试演的角色与排练时间。

人物：Ellis、Mo。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-16-s01 · Ellis: I'd like to join the play, but I'm shy around people I don't know.
- p06-b-16-s02 · Mo: We can start with a reading in a small group. You have a real talent for changing your voice.
- p06-b-16-s03 · Ellis: Does that mean you want me to play the stranger who arrives in scene two?
- p06-b-16-s04 · Mo: Possibly. His voice has to be different from everyone else's, and that's your strength.
- p06-b-16-s05 · Ellis: The others are so talented. I'm worried I'll slow them down.
- p06-b-16-s06 · Mo: They all need rehearsal too. Would you read the part with Nina and me on Tuesday at seven?
- p06-b-16-s07 · Ellis: Yes, I'd be comfortable with that. I'll read the scene beforehand.
- p06-b-16-s08 · Mo: Good. We'll try that role on Tuesday, then decide together whether it suits you.

目标用法：

- shy · adj. · 羞怯的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7bd17e32d525f71f-p06-b-16；来源 ox-7bd17e32d525f71f；证据 p06-b-16-s01；词形 shy。
- talent · n. · 天赋才能；义项 sense-staged-ox-21a56bcb0e027b7a-p06-b-16；来源 ox-21a56bcb0e027b7a；证据 p06-b-16-s02；词形 talent。
- stranger · n. · 陌生人；义项 sense-staged-ox-35df6c8b11d4fdcc-p06-b-16；来源 ox-35df6c8b11d4fdcc；证据 p06-b-16-s03；词形 stranger。
- strength · n. · 长处；义项 sense-staged-ox-979acd912b227f48-p06-b-16；来源 ox-979acd912b227f48；证据 p06-b-16-s04；词形 strength。
- talented · adj. · 有才能的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7914e8061c72f3ac-p06-b-16；来源 ox-7914e8061c72f3ac；证据 p06-b-16-s05；词形 talented。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Ellis's anxiety about unfamiliar people is met with a small reading group, and his voice-changing ability is tied to a particular role. The group agrees Tuesday at seven with preparation beforehand; the last turn carefully reserves final casting until after the trial. The title and goal describe this trial/discussion, not a guaranteed role assignment.

## 点一个按偏好制作的面包卷 · p06-b-17

主题：饮食沟通；情景：点购指定配料的三明治（cafe-sandwich-filling-order）。

- p06-b-17-s05 · Faye: Butter, please. Could you warm the bread slightly first? I don't want it toasted.

The customer specifies bread and fillings, asks about heat, rejects the chilli sauce and confirms gentle warming rather than toasting. This completes the order-specification goal without falsely claiming the food has already been served.

## 索取五十英镑预算内的印刷报价 · p06-b-18

主题：消费与服务；情景：索取预算内的印刷报价（printing-quote-scope-request）。

沟通目标：说明印刷需求及含税预算，约定店员发送完整报价

- p06-b-18-s01 · Sonia: Do you specialize in small print runs? I only need eighty leaflets.
- p06-b-18-s02 · Print Shop Owner: We do. What sort of paper and printing do you need?
- p06-b-18-s03 · Sonia: Specifically, I'd like colour on the front and plain text on the back. Ordinary paper is fine.
- p06-b-18-s04 · Print Shop Owner: Do you have a spending limit?
- p06-b-18-s05 · Sonia: My total budget is fifty pounds, including tax. I can collect them, so there's no delivery charge to add.
- p06-b-18-s06 · Print Shop Owner: Send me the file and I'll email a full quote. I won't start printing until you approve it.
- p06-b-18-s07 · Sonia: Thanks. I'll send the file today and wait for the quote before making a decision.

Sonia gives the eighty-leaflet quantity, two-sided requirements, ordinary paper and a fifty-pound tax-inclusive collection budget. ## 缩小就业调查标题的结论范围 · p06-b-19

主题：研究与论证；情景：限定就业调查标题的结论（employment-survey-headline-limits）。

沟通目标：核对样本与统计口径，删除无法支持的全市趋势结论

背景：两名学生审核一份虚构求职俱乐部调查的报告标题，避免把少量成员的情况写成全市就业趋势。

人物：Jin、Marta。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-19-s01 · Jin: At the start of this month, forty residents in our survey were unemployed. Eight have since found work. Can our headline say unemployment is falling across the city?
- p06-b-19-s02 · Marta: No. That's a statistic about one job club, not a measure of the whole city.
- p06-b-19-s03 · Jin: But doesn't it show a positive trend?
- p06-b-19-s04 · Marta: We only have this month's survey. We don't have a comparable result from last month.
- p06-b-19-s05 · Jin: And it's unlikely that this one club represents everyone looking for work.
- p06-b-19-s06 · Marta: Right. We should therefore keep the headline about the people we actually surveyed.
- p06-b-19-s07 · Jin: I'll change it to 'Eight survey participants found work this month' and explain the sample underneath.
- p06-b-19-s08 · Marta: That matches our evidence. Let's use that version.

目标用法：

## 请医生回电讨论反复喉咙痛 · p06-b-20

主题：健康与照护；情景：请求医生回电讨论喉咙不适（clinic-throat-callback-request）。

沟通目标：说明症状及持续时间，确认可接听时段并请接待员转交医生回电请求

背景：虚构就诊沟通练习：欧文向诊所说明反复出现的喉咙不适，希望医生评估是否需要治疗，接待员只安排回电。

人物：Owen、Clinic Receptionist。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-20-s01 · Owen: Could I speak to a doctor? I suffer from a sore throat most mornings, and it's been happening for two weeks.
- p06-b-20-s02 · Clinic Receptionist: I'll pass that on. Is that the main symptom you want to discuss?
- p06-b-20-s03 · Owen: Yes. It usually eases later in the day, but I'd like to know whether I need treatment.
- p06-b-20-s04 · Clinic Receptionist: The doctor will need to discuss it with you. Could you take a call between two and three today?
- p06-b-20-s05 · Owen: Yes, I'll keep my phone with me.
- p06-b-20-s06 · Clinic Receptionist: I'll request that callback and include what you've told me. If the doctor needs to see you, we'll arrange that after the call.

目标用法：

- suffer · v. · 受身体不适困扰；义项 sense-staged-ox-216c1483fab3bdf0-p06-b-20；来源 ox-216c1483fab3bdf0；证据 p06-b-20-s01；词形 suffer。

- symptom · n. · 症状；义项 sense-staged-ox-e0ff8ddd6eb0457c-p06-b-20；来源 ox-e0ff8ddd6eb0457c；证据 p06-b-20-s02；词形 symptom。
- treatment · n. · 治疗；义项 sense-staged-ox-9792773b165a045a-p06-b-20；来源 ox-9792773b165a045a；证据 p06-b-20-s03；词形 treatment。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Owen explains a repeated symptom, its two-week duration and daily pattern; the receptionist confines her role to relaying information and requesting a callback. He confirms availability from two to three, and she agrees to request it without guaranteeing a consultation or prescribing care. The fictional communication exercise meets the request/availability goal with no diagnostic conclusion.

## 说明牙痛并确认牙医预约 · p06-b-21

主题：健康与照护；情景：因牙痛预约牙医（dental-appointment-request）。

- p06-b-21-s04 · Dental Receptionist: I'll add that to your notes. Does tomorrow morning work for you?
- p06-b-21-s05 · Bianca: Yes. Will the dentist be able to treat it at that appointment?
- p06-b-21-s06 · Dental Receptionist: They'll examine you first and explain what can be done. I can't promise a particular treatment in advance.
- p06-b-21-s07 · Bianca: I understand. Please book me in for ten thirty tomorrow.
- p06-b-21-s08 · Dental Receptionist: That's booked. Please come to reception when you arrive.

目标用法：

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Bianca supplies onset, location and functional impact of tooth pain, accepts the offered ten-thirty appointment and receives explicit booking confirmation. The receptionist records details but does not promise immediate treatment, preserving the professional role boundary. The title's confirmed appointment and goal are supported by the final two turns, without requiring a diagnosis.

- p06-b-22-s03 · Nico: Please. This pair is too uncomfortable to wear all day.
- p06-b-22-s04 · Outdoor Shop Assistant: Here you are. The fitting room is still free.
- p06-b-22-s05 · Nico: These feel totally different. I can sit down comfortably now. Do I need the matching top as well?
- p06-b-22-s06 · Outdoor Shop Assistant: You said you already have a warm base layer for your upper body. Another one may be unnecessary.
- p06-b-22-s07 · Nico: Then I'll just take the larger long johns and use the top I already own.

The shopper reports an uncomfortable waist fit, tries the next size and confirms that sitting is now comfortable. The choice of larger long johns without an extra top meets the stated goal, with no completed checkout claimed.

- p06-b-23-s03 · Yara: Could I try it? I don't want a longer shoe just to get more room at the front.
- p06-b-23-s04 · Sports Shop Assistant: Certainly. Walk around in these and tell me how they feel.
- p06-b-23-s05 · Yara: Unlike the first pair, these don't squeeze my toes. My heels aren't slipping either.
- p06-b-23-s06 · Sports Shop Assistant: Would you like this pair?
- p06-b-23-s07 · Yara: Yes, I'll take the wider ones in this size.

Yara distinguishes adequate length from toe-width pressure, and the assistant offers the same size in a wider version. Walking establishes relief without heel slipping; she explicitly chooses the wider pair. The fitting sequence and title match the outcome, with shoe size and width kept consistently distinct.

## 选择远离湖岸的扎营位置 · p06-b-24

主题：文娱与运动；情景：选择湖边露营位置（lakeside-camp-location）。

沟通目标：比较营地位置并避开湖边危险区域。

背景：迪伦抵达湖边营地，向管理员比较两处空位，避免把帐篷搭在封闭湖岸附近。

- p06-b-24-s01 · Dylan: Can I put my tent on that flat patch beside the lake?
- p06-b-24-s02 · Campsite Warden: Not there. The ground beyond that rope is closed because part of the bank has collapsed.
- p06-b-24-s03 · Dylan: Is there another spot with a view of the water?
- p06-b-24-s04 · Campsite Warden: Try the southern edge of the field, beside the hedge. It's farther from the lake but sheltered from the wind.
- p06-b-24-s05 · Dylan: What about the open space near the gate?
- p06-b-24-s06 · Campsite Warden: You could camp there, but people walk through it all evening.
- p06-b-24-s07 · Dylan: I'll use the place by the hedge, then, and keep all my equipment on this side of the rope.
- p06-b-24-s08 · Campsite Warden: That works. The marked path from there leads to the water tap.

- spot · n. · 地点；义项 sense-staged-ox-1eccaa5a6160d75d-p06-b-24；来源 ox-1eccaa5a6160d75d；证据 p06-b-24-s03；词形 spot。
- southern · adj. · 南部的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5bdb0e01379f1c75-p06-b-24；来源 ox-5bdb0e01379f1c75；证据 p06-b-24-s04；词形 southern。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The warden explains the closed bank and offers a sheltered southern field edge; Dylan compares it with a permitted but busy gate area. He chooses the hedge location and explicitly keeps his equipment behind the rope, and the warden accepts the plan. The geographical title and lake-risk avoidance goal are therefore supported, while the tap direction is a useful closing detail.

## 先检查轮胎再决定是否更换 · p06-b-25

主题：消费与服务；情景：预约检查汽车轮胎（garage-tire-assessment-request）。

沟通目标：说明轮胎漏气现象并约定检查后再决定修补或更换

背景：韦斯已把汽车停在维修店外，轮胎反复漏气，他想先确认能否检查而不是直接购买新胎。

人物：Wes、Garage Receptionist。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-25-s01 · Wes: My front tire keeps losing air overnight. The car is outside. Can someone check it today?
- p06-b-25-s02 · Garage Receptionist: We can inspect it at two this afternoon. Have you noticed any damage?
- p06-b-25-s03 · Wes: No, and I only bought it last month. Surely I don't need a new one already?
- p06-b-25-s04 · Garage Receptionist: We can't tell until we've examined it. We'll call you with the findings and a price before doing any repairs.
- p06-b-25-s05 · Wes: That's fine. Please book the inspection for two. I'll leave the keys with you.
- p06-b-25-s06 · Garage Receptionist: Thank you. I'll put it on the schedule and make sure the mechanic knows to call first.

目标用法：

- tire · n. · 汽车轮胎（美式拼写）；义项 sense-staged-ox-15c055d3dfe965e6-p06-b-25；来源 ox-15c055d3dfe965e6；证据 p06-b-25-s01；词形 tire。
- surely · adv. · 表示相信某事应当如此并寻求认同；义项 sense-staged-ox-9f3b5867b5b1efd0-p06-b-25；来源 ox-9f3b5867b5b1efd0；证据 p06-b-25-s03；词形 surely。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Wes reports recurrent overnight air loss and asks for an inspection rather than authorizing a replacement. The receptionist offers two o'clock, declines to prejudge the fault and agrees to call with findings and price before repairs; Wes accepts and leaves the keys. This satisfies the inspection arrangement while preserving the later repair decision, and the American tire spelling has no conflicting tyre/labour spelling here.

- ox-15c055d3dfe965e6：accepted；s01: My front tire is a concrete count noun for the car's rubber wheel covering, clearly American spelling and not the verb become tired.（p06-b-25-s01）。
- ox-9f3b5867b5b1efd0：accepted；s03: Surely I don't need a new one already? is a sentence adverb expressing Wes's expectation and inviting agreement, grounded in the one-month age; the receptionist does not mistake that expectation for a verified conclusion.（p06-b-25-s03）。

## 确认自行车换胎与取车时间 · p06-b-26

主题：消费与服务；情景：询问自行车维修安排（bicycle-repair-estimate）。

沟通目标：检查车况、讨论维修必要性并确认取车时间。

背景：比娅把漏气的自行车送到维修店，维修师检查后说明需要更换的部件，两人商定取车安排。

人物：Bea、Cycle Mechanic。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-26-s01 · Cycle Mechanic: The tyre is badly worn, and the inner tube has a split near the valve.
- p06-b-26-s02 · Bea: Could you repair them rather than replace them?
- p06-b-26-s03 · Cycle Mechanic: The split won't hold a patch, and the outer rubber is cracking. I'd replace both. Parts and labour would be thirty pounds.
- p06-b-26-s04 · Bea: Okay, please do that. You said collection would be at five?
- p06-b-26-s05 · Cycle Mechanic: Yes, though I can have it ready by four if that helps.
- p06-b-26-s06 · Bea: Could we switch the collection time to four, then? I need to get home before five.
- p06-b-26-s07 · Cycle Mechanic: Four it is. I'll replace both parts and check the wheel before you arrive.

目标用法：

- tyre · n. · 轮胎（英式拼写）；义项 sense-staged-ox-10dc16681cb8ac4d-p06-b-26；来源 ox-10dc16681cb8ac4d；证据 p06-b-26-s01；词形 tyre。
- tube · n. · 充气内胎；义项 sense-staged-ox-a3afc2c9e4a9afa4-p06-b-26；来源 ox-a3afc2c9e4a9afa4；证据 p06-b-26-s01；词形 tube。
- switch · v. · 更换或改变安排；义项 sense-staged-ox-594bd949a9316ca6-p06-b-26；来源 ox-594bd949a9316ca6；证据 p06-b-26-s06；词形 switch。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The mechanic reports two separate defects and explains replacement rather than patching before giving an inclusive parts/labour price. Bea accepts both parts and negotiates four-o'clock collection, which the mechanic confirms. Her reference to the earlier five-o'clock time is compatible with an ongoing shop conversation and is immediately clarified; British tyre and labour spellings are consistent.

- ox-10dc16681cb8ac4d：accepted；s01: The tyre is badly worn names the bicycle's outer rubber component as a noun, using the selected British spelling and distinguishing it from the inner tube.（p06-b-26-s01）。
- ox-a3afc2c9e4a9afa4：accepted；s01: the inner tube has a split near the valve uses tube as the head noun of the conventional bicycle component name, with the valve and patch discussion confirming the inflatable-tube sense.（p06-b-26-s01）。
- ox-594bd949a9316ca6：accepted；s06: switch the collection time to four is a transitive verb for changing an agreed arrangement, with the new time as a to complement; it is not the noun device.（p06-b-26-s06）。

## 搬雕像前先评估展台 · p06-b-27

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：安排雕像展台评估与搬运（statue-display-moving-plan）。

沟通目标：说明雕像重量与现有底座问题，约定先评估底座再由专业人员搬运

背景：社区展馆即将接收一尊石雕，策展人莱奥妮和设施主管讨论旧展台的外观及承重问题，不自行移动重物。

人物：Leonie、Facilities Manager。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-27-s01 · Leonie: The delivery sheet says the statue weighs half a ton. Can we put it on the old display stand?
- p06-b-27-s02 · Facilities Manager: We don't know whether that stand can support it. We need a proper assessment before arranging the move.
- p06-b-27-s03 · Leonie: It's an ugly stand anyway, with that bright plastic cover. Could we use a different base?
- p06-b-27-s04 · Facilities Manager: Possibly, but appearance isn't the main issue. I'll ask our facilities engineer to assess the options, then get the specialist movers involved.
- p06-b-27-s05 · Leonie: Please do. I'll tell the delivery team to hold it at their warehouse until we have an approved base and a moving plan.

目标用法：

- statue · n. · 雕像；义项 sense-staged-ox-c1a054bceee948ee-p06-b-27；来源 ox-c1a054bceee948ee；证据 p06-b-27-s01；词形 statue。
- ton · n. · 吨（重量单位）；义项 sense-staged-ox-184011eb88c24bd0-p06-b-27；来源 ox-184011eb88c24bd0；证据 p06-b-27-s01；词形 ton。
- ugly · adj. · 难看的；义项 sense-staged-ox-9bceb5b8ea369d55-p06-b-27；来源 ox-9bceb5b8ea369d55；证据 p06-b-27-s03；词形 ugly。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The curator asks about a heavy delivery, while the facilities manager separates appearance preferences from unknown load capacity. They agree engineering assessment and specialist movers, and Leonie explicitly arranges to hold delivery until a base and plan are approved. The ending fulfils the planning objective without implying an unassessed stand is safe or that the statue has already moved.

- ox-184011eb88c24bd0：accepted；s01: weighs half a ton uses the count noun as a literal weight unit from the delivery sheet, not an informal large amount. No exact metric conversion is asserted, and only the selected B1 source ID is claimed.（p06-b-27-s01）。
- ox-9bceb5b8ea369d55：accepted；s03: an ugly stand uses the adjective for unattractive appearance, immediately explained by its bright plastic covering rather than suggesting danger or bad behaviour.（p06-b-27-s03）。

## 挑选适合初学者的纸牌魔术 · p06-b-28

主题：文娱与运动；情景：选择初学魔术练习（beginner-magic-practice-choice）。

沟通目标：根据练习难度选择无需快速手法的纸牌魔术并约定先看示范

背景：哈珀想在家庭聚会上表演一个简单魔术，但不擅长快速手部动作，向兴趣课教师询问练习选择。

人物：Harper、Magic Tutor。

起草：author-phase06-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-b-28-s01 · Harper: I'd like to learn a card trick, but my hands aren't quick enough for the one you just showed us.
- p06-b-28-s02 · Magic Tutor: Then try this one. In my beginner classes, people typically learn it in one session because it doesn't require fast movements.
- p06-b-28-s03 · Harper: Can I simply follow the steps on this sheet?
- p06-b-28-s04 · Magic Tutor: Use the sheet to practise, but watch a demonstration first. The order matters, and the pictures don't show every movement.
- p06-b-28-s05 · Harper: All right. Could you show me slowly, and then watch while I try?
- p06-b-28-s06 · Magic Tutor: Of course. Sit beside me so you can see the cards, and we'll go through it together.

目标用法：

- trick · n. · 魔术戏法；义项 sense-staged-ox-f4d6903607f8977f-p06-b-28；来源 ox-f4d6903607f8977f；证据 p06-b-28-s01；词形 trick。
- typically · adv. · 通常；义项 sense-staged-ox-3e585211e4734da4-p06-b-28；来源 ox-3e585211e4734da4；证据 p06-b-28-s02；词形 typically。
- simply · adv. · 仅仅只需；义项 sense-staged-ox-6ea322a9666e4503-p06-b-28；来源 ox-6ea322a9666e4503；证据 p06-b-28-s03；词形 simply。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Harper explains a dexterity constraint, receives a slower-movement alternative and asks whether the sheet alone will suffice. The tutor distinguishes practice instructions from a needed demonstration, and both agree to a slow showing followed by supervised trying. The goal is this learning arrangement, not mastery in one session; typically is properly scoped to the tutor's own classes.

绑定文件 SHA-256：2a21e0b9e0aff7a51029352e428d6e464eb2b5689d0c645a218f3fbfda71e390

- ox-f4d6903607f8977f：accepted；s01: a card trick is a count noun naming a magic performance, not a deception verb; the fast-hand-movement concern and tutor's demonstration establish the performance sense.（p06-b-28-s01）。
- ox-3e585211e4734da4：accepted；s02: people typically learn it in one session uses an adverb for a usual pattern in the stated beginner classes, not a guarantee about Harper or all learners.（p06-b-28-s02）。
- ox-6ea322a9666e4503：accepted；s03: Can I simply follow the steps on this sheet? uses the adverb to ask whether that action alone is sufficient. The reply requiring a demonstration supports the selected merely/only sense, rather than just easy execution.（p06-b-28-s03）。

## 决定购买适合练习的二手钢琴 · p06-c-01

主题：消费与服务；情景：挑选二手练习钢琴（secondhand-piano-purchase）。

沟通目标：核对二手钢琴的来源、声音和价格后决定是否购买

背景：娜迪娅在二手乐器店挑选练习用钢琴，格雷厄姆是店员。

人物：Nadia、Graham。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-01-s01 · Nadia: Is this a used piano, or has it only been on display?
- p06-c-01-s02 · Graham: Used. We bought it at an auction, then had our technician check and tune it.
- p06-c-01-s03 · Nadia: Is it a valuable antique? I need something to play, not something to collect.
- p06-c-01-s04 · Graham: No, it is an ordinary practice piano. You can try it now.
- p06-c-01-s05 · Nadia: The lower notes sound much clearer than on the other one. I value a clear sound more than an impressive label.
- p06-c-01-s06 · Graham: This one is four hundred pounds, including local delivery. The other one is three hundred.
- p06-c-01-s07 · Nadia: Four hundred is still affordable for me. Is the difference worth an extra hundred pounds?
- p06-c-01-s08 · Graham: Only if you prefer playing this one. The cheaper piano is not a bargain if you dislike its sound.
- p06-c-01-s09 · Nadia: Then I will take this one. Could you arrange delivery for next Thursday?
- p06-c-01-s10 · Graham: Yes. Thursday morning is available; I will put that on your order.

目标用法：

- used · adj. · 二手的，使用过的；义项 sense-staged-ox-1a13d28959904973-p06-c-01；来源 ox-1a13d28959904973；证据 p06-c-01-s01；词形 used。
- auction · n. · 拍卖；义项 sense-staged-ox-9bc9520c8f32c495-p06-c-01；来源 ox-9bc9520c8f32c495；证据 p06-c-01-s02；词形 auction。
- valuable · adj. · 值钱的，贵重的；义项 sense-staged-ox-cd28af7782092e5a-p06-c-01；来源 ox-cd28af7782092e5a；证据 p06-c-01-s03；词形 valuable。
- value · v. · 重视，珍视；义项 sense-staged-ox-e533c5b2ea17ff9d-p06-c-01；来源 ox-e533c5b2ea17ff9d；证据 p06-c-01-s05；词形 value。
- affordable · adj. · 负担得起的；义项 sense-staged-ox-129ea990020b3b6d-p06-c-01；来源 ox-129ea990020b3b6d；证据 p06-c-01-s07；词形 affordable。
- worth · adj. · 值得某个价钱的；义项 sense-staged-ox-b4307dd69f6e4363-p06-c-01；来源 ox-b4307dd69f6e4363；证据 p06-c-01-s07；词形 worth。
- bargain · n. · 价格划算的东西；义项 sense-staged-ox-b0c5bf6641e982c5-p06-c-01；来源 ox-b0c5bf6641e982c5；证据 p06-c-01-s08；词形 bargain。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The shop enquiry establishes the instrument is second-hand and technician-checked, compares sound and delivered prices, and ends with Nadia choosing the clearer-sounding piano and Graham booking Thursday morning. The services classification, purchasing title and goal all match; the seller avoids implausible antique claims.

- ox-9bc9520c8f32c495：accepted；s02 at an auction names the sale event from which the shop acquired the piano; the indefinite article and preposition establish the selected noun.（p06-c-01-s02）。

## 把报告中的增长改为同口径比较 · p06-c-02

主题：工作与协作；情景：核对报告版本与数据口径（report-data-reconciliation）。

沟通目标：查明两个报告数字不同的原因并约定修订可比较的数据和说明

背景：分析员萨米尔与项目同事埃莉斯检查一份虚构月度报告，发现图表可能使用了不同数据范围。

人物：Samir、Elise。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-02-s01 · Samir: Which version did you send to the team? My chart ends in June, but yours includes July.
- p06-c-02-s02 · Elise: I sent the file you shared this morning. I thought the extra column had been checked.
- p06-c-02-s03 · Samir: As the analyst, I should have marked it as unfinished. July includes two shops that were not in the June total.
- p06-c-02-s04 · Elise: So the apparent rise might just reflect the extra shops?
- p06-c-02-s05 · Samir: Yes. We need the actual sales figures for the same shops in both months.
- p06-c-02-s06 · Elise: I can copy those figures from the shop reports. Will you check that I have entered them accurately?
- p06-c-02-s07 · Samir: Of course. Accuracy matters more than getting this out before lunch.
- p06-c-02-s08 · Elise: And we should state our assumption that every shop recorded returns in the same way.
- p06-c-02-s09 · Samir: Agreed. Replace the chart, add that note, and send the draft back to me before it goes to the team.

- version · n. · 某文件的版本；义项 sense-staged-ox-5e9f8e997712fd78-p06-c-02；来源 ox-5e9f8e997712fd78；证据 p06-c-02-s01；词形 version。
- analyst · n. · 分析员；义项 sense-staged-ox-c415fd9ebdb695d2-p06-c-02；来源 ox-c415fd9ebdb695d2；证据 p06-c-02-s03；词形 analyst。
- apparent · adj. · 表面上的，看似存在的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5c854ec9a1e58f91-p06-c-02；来源 ox-5c854ec9a1e58f91；证据 p06-c-02-s04；词形 apparent。
- actual · adj. · 实际的，真实的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ea72c4d72bc2a89b-p06-c-02；来源 ox-ea72c4d72bc2a89b；证据 p06-c-02-s05；词形 actual。
- accurately · adv. · 准确地；义项 sense-staged-ox-989bbc41f24cf2da-p06-c-02；来源 ox-989bbc41f24cf2da；证据 p06-c-02-s06；词形 accurately。
- accuracy · n. · 准确性；义项 sense-staged-ox-2786329da7f7121b-p06-c-02；来源 ox-2786329da7f7121b；证据 p06-c-02-s07；词形 accuracy。
- assumption · n. · 未经证实而暂时采用的假设；义项 sense-staged-ox-7e492b8d0e0fa04e-p06-c-02；来源 ox-7e492b8d0e0fa04e；证据 p06-c-02-s08；词形 assumption。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The analysts identify a real comparability issue, namely July adding two shops, and agree a same-shop replacement chart, a returns assumption and a review-before-resending workflow. Each speaker maintains a consistent role and the work/report-reconciliation goal is achieved by an explicit agreed revision plan.

- ox-5c854ec9a1e58f91：accepted；s04 apparent rise describes growth that only seems to exist because coverage changed; the question and subsequent correction support the seeming adjective sense.（p06-c-02-s04）。

## 改看战后重建的纪录片一集 · p06-c-03

主题：文娱与运动；情景：选择适合共同观看的纪录片集数（documentary-family-viewing-choice）。

背景：两位家长在家庭观影前查看战争纪录片的分集说明，希望避开战斗画面。

- p06-c-03-s08 · Colin: No. It focuses on the teachers and the children returning to class.
- p06-c-03-s09 · Mara: Then let us watch episode three together and leave the first one for another evening.

## 在一小时内参观航空展厅 · p06-c-04

主题：文娱与运动；情景：确定航空展参观路线（aviation-museum-route）。

沟通目标：根据展品位置和可用时间选定航空展厅的参观路线

背景：西奥向航空博物馆工作人员露丝询问旧飞机和机翼展品的位置。

人物：Theo、Ruth。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-04-s01 · Theo: I wonder which hall has the early passenger aircraft. We have to leave within an hour.
- p06-c-04-s02 · Ruth: Go to the western hall. The red arrow on this map points to its entrance.
- p06-c-04-s03 · Theo: Is that where I can see inside a wing? I saw a photograph of one with the cover removed.
- p06-c-04-s04 · Ruth: Yes. That display stands alongside the full-size aircraft, so you can look at both in the same hall.
- p06-c-04-s05 · Theo: Will we have time for the upstairs gallery as well?
- p06-c-04-s06 · Ruth: Probably not if you want to stop and read the labels.
- p06-c-04-s07 · Theo: We will stay in the western hall, then. Could you show me the quickest way back to this exit?
- p06-c-04-s08 · Ruth: Take this corridor straight back. You do not need to go upstairs.

## 约好改短羊毛外套袖子 · p06-c-05

主题：消费与服务；情景：约定外套改尺寸（wool-coat-alteration-plan）。

沟通目标：根据试穿结果确定外套袖长的调整并约好取件

背景：伊内兹带着过长的羊毛外套到裁缝店，请裁缝保罗评估袖长。

人物：Inez、Paul。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-05-s01 · Inez: Can you alter this wool coat? The sleeves cover half my hands.
- p06-c-05-s02 · Paul: Certainly. Put it on, and I will mark the length with pins.
- p06-c-05-s03 · Inez: Could you adjust the left sleeve a little more? It still feels longer than the right one.
- p06-c-05-s04 · Paul: Try lowering both arms. How does it feel now?
- p06-c-05-s05 · Inez: Much better. Please keep them at the lengths you have marked.
- p06-c-05-s06 · Paul: I can have it ready on Friday afternoon. The work will cost twenty pounds.
- p06-c-05-s07 · Inez: That is fine. I will come after three on Friday.

- alter · v. · 改动衣服使其合身；义项 sense-staged-ox-2dd25d0bbb3fa8c4-p06-c-05；来源 ox-2dd25d0bbb3fa8c4；证据 p06-c-05-s01；词形 alter。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The fitting starts from overlong sleeves, resolves perceived left/right imbalance by checking arm position, then fixes the pinned lengths, price and Friday pickup. The customer/tailor exchange is coherent and achieves the alteration agreement claimed by the services scenario, without pretending the sewing is already done.

## 明确社区活动入口和接待分工 · p06-c-06

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：安排社区活动迎宾（community-event-welcome）。

沟通目标：确认入口与电梯位置并分配接待工作

- p06-c-06-s01 · Yasmin: You are our new volunteer, right? Here is a badge so visitors know they can ask you for help.
- p06-c-06-s02 · Joel: Thanks. Should I stand by the front steps?
- p06-c-06-s03 · Yasmin: Use the side entrance instead. People arrive with various needs, and that entrance leads straight to the lift.
- p06-c-06-s04 · Joel: Can the lift accommodate someone with a wheelchair and a companion?
- p06-c-06-s05 · Yasmin: Yes, both can travel together. Please ask before offering any physical assistance.
- p06-c-06-s06 · Joel: Who should I contact if someone needs more help than I can give?
- p06-c-06-s07 · Yasmin: I will assign Lian to the lift area. Call her whenever you are unsure; she knows the building well.
- p06-c-06-s08 · Joel: All right. I will welcome people at the side door and direct them to Lian when needed.
- p06-c-06-s09 · Yasmin: Exactly. I will stay at the registration desk just inside that door.

## 表决采用活动室清洁轮值表 · p06-c-07

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：表决活动室清洁安排（association-meeting-decision）。

沟通目标：居民协会讨论清洁轮值方案并明确采用决定和后续说明责任

背景：小型居民协会的三名委员全部到场，讨论由协会自行管理的活动室清洁安排。

人物：Bea、Kofi、Lin。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-07-s01 · Bea: The next item on our agenda is the cleaning rota for the shared room.
- p06-c-07-s02 · Kofi: Does the association need the building manager's approval for that?
- p06-c-07-s03 · Bea: Not for cleaning. Our agreement lets us manage this room, but changes to the building still need permission.
- p06-c-07-s04 · Lin: Then can we approve the rota today? Every household has already agreed to its week.
- p06-c-07-s05 · Kofi: I support it, as long as people can swap weeks when problems arise.
- p06-c-07-s06 · Bea: That is in the note at the bottom. Shall we take a vote on whether to adopt this rota? I am in favour.
- p06-c-07-s07 · Kofi: Yes from me too.
- p06-c-07-s08 · Lin: And me. I will put the rota on the noticeboard and explain how to arrange a swap.
- p06-c-07-s09 · Bea: Thank you. That is agreed by all three of us.

All three committee members are present, establish their authority to manage cleaning, retain an agreed swap provision, each voice assent, and assign Lin the posting/explanation. The final unanimous decision is grounded in the actual dialogue, with no unsupported full-association vote; the community cleaning-rota title and goal fit.
