## 承认拿错笔记本并为误指责道歉 · p06-c-08

主题：家庭与人际；情景：纠正误指责并道歉（misplaced-notebook-apology）。

沟通目标：承认自己拿错物品造成误会并向朋友道歉，约定归还物品

背景：埃文发现自己误拿了罗莎的笔记本，此前却指责罗莎拿走了他的。

人物：Evan、Rosa。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-08-s01 · Evan: Rosa, I found your notebook in my bag. I accidentally picked it up after class.
- p06-c-08-s02 · Rosa: Then why did you tell everyone I had taken yours? I was really upset.
- p06-c-08-s03 · Evan: I had no right to accuse you. I saw an empty space on my desk and jumped to a conclusion.
- p06-c-08-s04 · Rosa: It was not just the missing notebook. You would not listen when I explained.
- p06-c-08-s05 · Evan: I need to acknowledge that too. My anger was not an excuse to speak to you like that.
- p06-c-08-s06 · Rosa: I appreciate you saying so, but the others still think I took it.
- p06-c-08-s07 · Evan: I am ashamed of that. You deserve an apology in front of the same people. May I explain what happened before class tomorrow?
- p06-c-08-s08 · Rosa: Yes, please. And bring my notebook; I need it for the lesson.
- p06-c-08-s09 · Evan: I will. I will also make clear that none of this was your fault.

目标用法：

- accidentally · adv. · 意外地，无意中；义项 sense-staged-ox-ef845f10d2426634-p06-c-08；来源 ox-ef845f10d2426634；证据 p06-c-08-s01；词形 accidentally。
- upset · adj. · 难过的，不快的；义项 sense-staged-ox-60118e3eb8e04901-p06-c-08；来源 ox-60118e3eb8e04901；证据 p06-c-08-s02；词形 upset。
- accuse · v. · 指责某人做错事；义项 sense-staged-ox-044988fbf6a2f78b-p06-c-08；来源 ox-044988fbf6a2f78b；证据 p06-c-08-s03；词形 accuse。
- acknowledge · v. · 承认事实或问题；义项 sense-staged-ox-45d105e4e38aa198-p06-c-08；来源 ox-45d105e4e38aa198；证据 p06-c-08-s05；词形 acknowledge。
- anger · n. · 愤怒；义项 sense-staged-ox-734e54ce6ae247c4-p06-c-08；来源 ox-734e54ce6ae247c4；证据 p06-c-08-s05；词形 anger。
- ashamed · adj. · 感到羞愧的；义项 sense-staged-ox-424d223e21cda03a-p06-c-08；来源 ox-424d223e21cda03a；证据 p06-c-08-s07；词形 ashamed。
- apology · n. · 道歉；义项 sense-staged-ox-d5c4f9bfee7e2786-p06-c-08；来源 ox-d5c4f9bfee7e2786；证据 p06-c-08-s07；词形 apology。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Evan admits the mistaken pickup and baseless accusation, acknowledges that anger does not excuse it, and accepts Rosa's request for public correction and notebook return. The remorse and repair agreement support the relationships/apology scenario; Rosa does not unrealistically treat a private admission as a complete public remedy.

## 先借笔记试讲展示开场 · p06-c-09

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定先练习课堂展示的开场（student-presentation-support-plan）。

沟通目标：说明公开发言顾虑，并同意借助笔记向同伴试讲开场

背景：尼科准备课堂展示时感到紧张，同学阿莎提出先听他试讲开场。

人物：Niko、Asha。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-09-s01 · Niko: I feel anxious every time I imagine standing in front of the class.
- p06-c-09-s02 · Asha: Is it remembering your points, or having everyone look at you?
- p06-c-09-s03 · Niko: Both. The anxiety gets worse when I lose my place, and then there is an awkward silence.
- p06-c-09-s04 · Asha: Could you put the first three points on a card and try the opening with me?
- p06-c-09-s05 · Niko: Yes, but please do not correct me halfway through.
- p06-c-09-s06 · Asha: I can assure you that I will wait until you finish. We can talk about it afterward.
- p06-c-09-s07 · Niko: Thanks. Would you also accompany me to the classroom tomorrow?
- p06-c-09-s08 · Asha: Of course. For now, get your notes and start whenever you are ready.
- p06-c-09-s09 · Niko: All right. Here is my card. I will try just the first minute.

## 用信件限定祖辈移民展板的描述 · p06-c-10

主题：学习与技能；情景：修订家族移民经历展览说明（family-migration-exhibit-text）。

沟通目标：核对人物经历与历史材料，限定有证据支持的影响描述并安排补充出处

背景：卢卡正在为家族移民经历课堂展览撰写说明，海伦老师与他一起查看家族保存的信件和日期。

人物：Luca、Helen。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-10-s01 · Luca: I want this panel to be about my ancestor Elena, who moved here in 1912.
- p06-c-10-s02 · Helen: Which part of her life can you support with these letters?
- p06-c-10-s03 · Luca: Her youth in the new town. She was nineteen when she arrived, and she describes finding work at a laundry.
- p06-c-10-s04 · Helen: Then focus on that aspect of her experience. Your draft says she changed the whole town, but the letters do not show that.
- p06-c-10-s05 · Luca: You are right. They tell us what happened to her, not what happened to everyone else.
- p06-c-10-s06 · Helen: Keep the arrival date and the laundry job. Add the dates of the letters beside those details.
- p06-c-10-s07 · Luca: I will remove the claim about the town and put a source note under each quotation.

- ancestor · n. · 祖先，祖辈；义项 sense-staged-ox-1d08564adc1fc073-p06-c-10；来源 ox-1d08564adc1fc073；证据 p06-c-10-s01；词形 ancestor。
- youth · n. · 青年时期；义项 sense-staged-ox-414dc645e131023a-p06-c-10；来源 ox-414dc645e131023a；证据 p06-c-10-s03；词形 youth。
- aspect · n. · 某事物的一个方面；义项 sense-staged-ox-3247b2bb619c10c2-p06-c-10；来源 ox-3247b2bb619c10c2；证据 p06-c-10-s04；词形 aspect。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The teacher distinguishes a documented individual migration story from an unsupported whole-town impact claim. Luca agrees to remove the overclaim and cite the dated letters, fulfilling the classroom exhibition revision goal; family history content is appropriately framed as learning rather than an unrelated family conversation.

- ox-1d08564adc1fc073：accepted；s01 my ancestor Elena names an earlier family member with a possessive and proper-name apposition, establishing the family-line noun sense.（p06-c-10-s01）。
- ox-414dc645e131023a：accepted；s03 Her youth denotes Elena's early period of life, supported by arrival at nineteen; it does not denote a young person.（p06-c-10-s03）。

## 询问成瘾支持服务的首次联系办法 · p06-c-11

主题：健康与照护；情景：询问成瘾支持服务（addiction-support-inquiry）。

沟通目标：了解支持机构的联络方式及费用援助咨询渠道，并确定首次联系的下一步

背景：虚构沟通练习中，费伊向社区服务接待员询问成瘾支持的联络信息，不讨论个人诊断或治疗方案。

人物：Faye、Receptionist Owen。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-11-s01 · Faye: I am looking for someone to talk to about addiction. Is this the right desk?
- p06-c-11-s02 · Receptionist Owen: I can give you the contact details for the local support agency. Their adviser can explain what they offer.
- p06-c-11-s03 · Faye: I am also worried about the cost. Is there any financial aid?
- p06-c-11-s04 · Receptionist Owen: I do not know which arrangements would apply to you. The adviser can answer that before you book anything.
- p06-c-11-s05 · Faye: Could I have their number on paper? I would rather call from home.
- p06-c-11-s06 · Receptionist Owen: Certainly. Here it is, with the opening hours.
- p06-c-11-s07 · Faye: Thank you. I will call and ask about an initial conversation and the costs.

- aid · n. · 援助，帮助；义项 sense-staged-ox-f39c6b33aaffbc9b-p06-c-11；来源 ox-f39c6b33aaffbc9b；证据 p06-c-11-s03；词形 aid。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Faye asks about support and affordability, the receptionist explicitly avoids guessing eligibility, hands over contact details and hours, and Faye chooses to call from home. This achieves the health-service information/first-contact goal without supplying a diagnosis or treatment claim; named speaker identities remain stable.

- p06-c-12-s04 · Miles: I will replace it with a photograph of the venue. Shall we mention the workshops under the main talk?
- p06-c-12-s05 · Sana: Yes. The main talk and the associated activities are all free. That needs to be clear.

The organisers catch a wrong professional title, replace an assumptive image, clarify that all related activities are free, and route the draft to the agreed nurse reviewer before printing. This coherently completes a community-event poster revision plan, not a clinical discussion; the final draft status preserves the pending health-content check.

## 选择面向工作交流的口语试课 · p06-c-13

主题：学习与技能；情景：选择适合初学者的口语班（language-tutor-practice-plan）。

沟通目标：确认教师、练习内容和每周课程时间

背景：君想提高与商店顾客交谈的能力，向贝丝老师询问初学口语班。

人物：Jun、Tutor Beth。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-13-s01 · Jun: I want to acquire the language skills I need to talk to customers. Is your class suitable for a beginner?
- p06-c-13-s02 · Tutor Beth: Yes. I teach it myself. What would you like to accomplish in the first month?
- p06-c-13-s03 · Jun: I would like to explain where things are and answer simple questions about prices. I am not trying to copy a particular accent.
- p06-c-13-s04 · Tutor Beth: That is a useful goal. My approach is to practise short conversations, then repeat them with a small change.
- p06-c-13-s05 · Jun: Could we use situations from my shop rather than a restaurant?
- p06-c-13-s06 · Tutor Beth: Certainly. I can adapt the activities to your work. A few minutes of practice between classes would be beneficial too.
- p06-c-13-s07 · Jun: When does the class meet?
- p06-c-13-s08 · Tutor Beth: Every Tuesday at six. You can attend one trial lesson before deciding about the full course.
- p06-c-13-s09 · Jun: I will come next Tuesday at six, then. I can bring examples of questions customers ask.

- acquire · v. · 获得，逐渐学到技能；义项 sense-staged-ox-8fae22bb05f3395e-p06-c-13；来源 ox-8fae22bb05f3395e；证据 p06-c-13-s01；词形 acquire。

- accent · n. · 口音；义项 sense-staged-ox-9ea0ff59cb0ba886-p06-c-13；来源 ox-9ea0ff59cb0ba886；证据 p06-c-13-s03；词形 accent。

- beneficial · adj. · 有益的；义项 sense-staged-ox-2db35ae053d9c311-p06-c-13；来源 ox-2db35ae053d9c311；证据 p06-c-13-s06；词形 beneficial。

Jun explains a concrete customer-service learning aim, Beth identifies herself as teacher and proposes adaptable practice, and they settle on a Tuesday-six trial. The goal of confirming teacher, content and weekly time is fully supported; the title accurately limits the commitment to a trial rather than purchase of a full course.

## 核对总价和取消条件后预订课程住宿 · p06-c-14

主题：出行与住宿；情景：核对短期课程住宿并预订（course-stay-price-and-arrival）。

沟通目标：比较住宿总价，核对取消条款和抵达时间后确认预订

背景：佩特拉准备参加短期课程，向住宿接待员丹尼尔比较两种四晚住宿方案。

人物：Petra、Host Daniel。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-14-s01 · Petra: Could you tell me whether the course rate includes breakfast?
- p06-c-14-s02 · Host Daniel: The room-only rate is fifty pounds a night. Breakfast costs an additional eight pounds each morning. Our breakfast package is fifty-five pounds a night.
- p06-c-14-s03 · Petra: Four nights with breakfast added separately would be two hundred and thirty-two pounds, then. The package would be two hundred and twenty for the same stay?
- p06-c-14-s04 · Host Daniel: Correct. Additionally, that package includes the evening shuttle back from the college. There are no other fees.
- p06-c-14-s05 · Petra: That total is acceptable. Do I have to pay in advance?
- p06-c-14-s06 · Host Daniel: No. Payment is due upon arrival. You can cancel without a charge until noon two days before arrival; after that, the first night is charged.
- p06-c-14-s07 · Petra: I arrive on Monday the twelfth, probably around nine in the evening. Is that too late?
- p06-c-14-s08 · Host Daniel: No, our reception is open until ten. Would you like the package for four nights from that Monday?
- p06-c-14-s09 · Petra: Yes, please book it under Petra Novak. I understand the cancellation charge.
- p06-c-14-s10 · Host Daniel: It is booked. I will email the written confirmation with the dates, total, and cancellation terms.
- p06-c-14-s11 · Petra: Thank you. I will check the email and let you know if my arrival time changes.

- whether · conj. · 是否；义项 sense-staged-ox-1e462dabd7cbd9d6-p06-c-14；来源 ox-1e462dabd7cbd9d6；证据 p06-c-14-s01；词形 whether。

- additionally · adv. · 此外；义项 sense-staged-ox-c3b41f3e37f6b36f-p06-c-14；来源 ox-c3b41f3e37f6b36f；证据 p06-c-14-s04；词形 additionally。
- acceptable · adj. · 可接受的；义项 sense-staged-ox-a30756592a08e8a1-p06-c-14；来源 ox-a30756592a08e8a1；证据 p06-c-14-s05；词形 acceptable。

- written · adj. · 书面的；义项 sense-staged-ox-8b0552a4f251864a-p06-c-14；来源 ox-8b0552a4f251864a；证据 p06-c-14-s10；词形 written。

The host presents two rates and the arithmetic correctly yields 232 versus 220 pounds for four nights. Petra checks extras, payment, cancellation and late arrival, explicitly requests the package, and receives a booked confirmation with written terms to follow. The travel scenario genuinely reaches a booking outcome, with no contradiction between pay-on-arrival and a later cancellation charge.

## 调整画廊灯光以保留画作效果 · p06-c-15

主题：工作与协作；情景：调整展览画作照明（gallery-exhibition-lighting-plan）。

沟通目标：比较画作在不同照明角度下的观看效果并确定安装调整

背景：策展人梅与灯光安装员托马斯在布展现场查看一幅抽象画。

人物：Curator Mei、Installer Tomas。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-15-s01 · Curator Mei: This abstract painting looks quite different under the artificial light. The pale shapes almost disappear.
- p06-c-15-s02 · Installer Tomas: The lamp is pointing straight at the surface. I can change the angle and let you compare.
- p06-c-15-s03 · Curator Mei: Please do. The artist wants the shapes to emerge slowly; that is an artistic choice we need to preserve.
- p06-c-15-s04 · Installer Tomas: How about this position? Stand by the doorway and see what a visitor would see.
- p06-c-15-s05 · Curator Mei: That is better. The pale shapes are visible now, and there is no bright reflection across the artwork.
- p06-c-15-s06 · Installer Tomas: Shall I secure the lamp here?
- p06-c-15-s07 · Curator Mei: Yes. Keep this position, and I will check it once more after the other room lights are on.

目标用法：

- abstract · adj. · 抽象的，非写实的；义项 sense-staged-ox-9eba8c0b7804f31b-p06-c-15；来源 ox-9eba8c0b7804f31b；证据 p06-c-15-s01；词形 abstract。
- artificial · adj. · 人工的，非自然产生的；义项 sense-staged-ox-2341bbe8ccbd8277-p06-c-15；来源 ox-2341bbe8ccbd8277；证据 p06-c-15-s01；词形 artificial。
- angle · n. · 角度；义项 sense-staged-ox-44163938b804a981-p06-c-15；来源 ox-44163938b804a981；证据 p06-c-15-s02；词形 angle。
- artistic · adj. · 艺术上的；义项 sense-staged-ox-3f66e579f2f5f2e6-p06-c-15；来源 ox-3f66e579f2f5f2e6；证据 p06-c-15-s03；词形 artistic。
- artwork · n. · 艺术作品；义项 sense-staged-ox-e630884174f85a7d-p06-c-15；来源 ox-e630884174f85a7d；证据 p06-c-15-s05；词形 artwork。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The curator identifies loss of pale shapes and the installer alters lamp direction, allowing a real before/after comparison from the doorway. Mei authorises securing the improved position and adds a later whole-room check. The work classification and installation-adjustment goal are justified by their professional roles and an actual agreed setting.

- ox-2341bbe8ccbd8277：accepted；s01 artificial light denotes lamp-produced rather than natural illumination, with the lamp identified immediately afterward.（p06-c-15-s01）。

## 让科幻动画的开场交代清楚来访者 · p06-c-16

主题：工作与协作；情景：根据反馈调整影片开头（short-film-feedback）。

沟通目标：剪辑者根据试映反馈调整短片开头。

背景：导演卓娅看完剪辑师本的科幻动画新版本后，讨论如何让观众理解开场的来访角色。

人物：Director Zoya、Editor Ben。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-16-s01 · Director Zoya: The animation is beautiful, but I thought the visitor was a ghost until the spaceship appeared.
- p06-c-16-s02 · Editor Ben: I wanted the alien to seem mysterious. Should I show the ship sooner?
- p06-c-16-s03 · Director Zoya: Yes. The audience needs to know this is a living being from another planet, not someone returning from the dead.
- p06-c-16-s04 · Editor Ben: Would a brief view of the ship before the door opens be enough?
- p06-c-16-s05 · Director Zoya: I think so. The door opening onto a whole forest is an astonishing image; keep that reveal.
- p06-c-16-s06 · Editor Ben: And the audio track? At the moment there is a whisper before we see anything.
- p06-c-16-s07 · Director Zoya: Replace the whisper with the engine slowing down. Save the amusing sneeze for when the visitor steps into the flowers.
- p06-c-16-s08 · Editor Ben: That gives the sneeze a reason. I will add the ship first, change the sound, and keep the forest reveal.
- p06-c-16-s09 · Director Zoya: Good. Let us watch the revised opening together before you work on the next scene.

- animation · n. · 动画影像或动画制作；义项 sense-staged-ox-7df45a6f242a741e-p06-c-16；来源 ox-7df45a6f242a741e；证据 p06-c-16-s01；词形 animation。
- alien · n. · 外星生物；义项 sense-staged-ox-65a10b20ec469fe6-p06-c-16；来源 ox-65a10b20ec469fe6；证据 p06-c-16-s02；词形 alien。
- being · n. · 生物，有生命的存在；义项 sense-staged-ox-91e9a30d1f851d90-p06-c-16；来源 ox-91e9a30d1f851d90；证据 p06-c-16-s03；词形 being。
- astonishing · adj. · 令人惊讶的；义项 sense-staged-ox-eb2cc879773f4de3-p06-c-16；来源 ox-eb2cc879773f4de3；证据 p06-c-16-s05；词形 astonishing。
- audio · adj. · 声音的，音频的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ada97d0cc05b37cf-p06-c-16；来源 ox-ada97d0cc05b37cf；证据 p06-c-16-s06；词形 audio。
- amusing · adj. · 有趣好笑的；义项 sense-staged-ox-847943b3fd8b351f-p06-c-16；来源 ox-847943b3fd8b351f；证据 p06-c-16-s07；词形 amusing。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The director identifies a specific mistaken ghost interpretation and the editor proposes showing the ship earlier; they agree matching engine audio, a motivated sneeze and preservation of the forest reveal. The work/short-film feedback scenario culminates in an explicit editing plan and review checkpoint, not a claim that unshown edits are already complete.

- ox-91e9a30d1f851d90：accepted；s03 a living being is a noun phrase with article and adjective, meaning an organism rather than the participle of be.（p06-c-16-s03）。

## 把实验图表结论限制在观察范围内 · p06-c-17

主题：学习与技能；情景：修订课堂实验结果说明（classroom-culture-results-review）。

沟通目标：依据所给实验图表区分观察与概括，并补充报告的限制

背景：阿米拉与老师讨论一份虚构课堂练习的现成图表，没有实际培养微生物或操作化学品。

人物：Student Amira、Teacher Lewis。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-17-s01 · Student Amira: My conclusion says the acid killed all the bacteria. Does that match the chart?
- p06-c-17-s02 · Teacher Lewis: Look at the heading. The chart shows slower growth in one sample, not the death of every organism.
- p06-c-17-s03 · Student Amira: So I have described something that was not measured.
- p06-c-17-s04 · Teacher Lewis: Exactly. A balanced report should explain both the result and what this single test cannot tell us.
- p06-c-17-s05 · Student Amira: I will write that growth was slower in this sample and remove the claim that everything died.
- p06-c-17-s06 · Teacher Lewis: Good. Also identify the sample and time period given in the worksheet.
- p06-c-17-s07 · Student Amira: I will add those details and say that we cannot generalize from this one chart.

目标用法：

- acid · n. · 酸，酸性化学物质；义项 sense-staged-ox-98ffe49d4ee44577-p06-c-17；来源 ox-98ffe49d4ee44577；证据 p06-c-17-s01；词形 acid。
- bacteria · n. · 细菌；义项 sense-staged-ox-53003e54e165666d-p06-c-17；来源 ox-53003e54e165666d；证据 p06-c-17-s01；词形 bacteria。
- balanced · adj. · 全面而不过度偏向某一方面的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7068bfe4c5946249-p06-c-17；来源 ox-7068bfe4c5946249；证据 p06-c-17-s04；词形 balanced。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The teacher corrects the exact overclaim that slower observed growth proves universal death, and Amira agrees a narrower conclusion with sample/time details and a limitation statement. The learning scenario works as interpretation of a supplied chart, not a real experiment protocol; the title and outcome are evidence-bounded.

- ox-98ffe49d4ee44577：accepted；s01 the acid is a definite noun phrase naming the chemical substance in the student's draft; later correction disputes its claimed effect, not the word sense.（p06-c-17-s01）。
- ox-53003e54e165666d：accepted；s01 all the bacteria is the plural microorganism noun as the object of killed in a reported overclaim, clearly disambiguated by organism and growth in s02.（p06-c-17-s01）。

## 安排救助物资的临时存放和称重 · p06-c-18

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：分配洪水救助物资整理工作（flood-supply-sorting）。

沟通目标：确认临时物资间和大件捐赠物的安放位置

背景：洪水退去后，未受淹的山上社区中心整理捐赠物资，负责人向新帮手说明存放安排。

人物：Coordinator Pilar、Helper Dan。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-18-s01 · Coordinator Pilar: We are using the dry basement as a temporary store. There is adequate space there for the boxes, but leave the stairs clear.
- p06-c-18-s02 · Helper Dan: What about the donated tables outside? They will not fit through the stairwell.
- p06-c-18-s03 · Coordinator Pilar: Keep those in the ground-floor hall. The collection team will take them directly from there.
- p06-c-18-s04 · Helper Dan: Are these food boxes adequately labelled? I can see the contents, but not the dates.
- p06-c-18-s05 · Coordinator Pilar: Not yet. Put them aside for the team checking dates. For now, could you weigh this basket of blankets?
- p06-c-18-s06 · Helper Dan: Yes. The basket is too heavy for that small scale, though.
- p06-c-18-s07 · Coordinator Pilar: Use the trolley scale by the door. Then write the weight on the collection sheet.
- p06-c-18-s08 · Helper Dan: Understood. Tables stay in the hall, boxes await the date check, and I will start with the blankets.

目标用法：

- basement · n. · 地下室；义项 sense-staged-ox-f15dd8db49611a27-p06-c-18；来源 ox-f15dd8db49611a27；证据 p06-c-18-s01；词形 basement。
- adequate · adj. · 足够的，能满足需要的；义项 sense-staged-ox-4fc64664c1d9b1f4-p06-c-18；来源 ox-4fc64664c1d9b1f4；证据 p06-c-18-s01；词形 adequate。
- adequately · adv. · 充分地，达到要求地；义项 sense-staged-ox-846d3ce96aa7a2b9-p06-c-18；来源 ox-846d3ce96aa7a2b9；证据 p06-c-18-s04；词形 adequately。
- weigh · v. · 称重量；义项 sense-staged-ox-d44c00bef6968857-p06-c-18；来源 ox-d44c00bef6968857；证据 p06-c-18-s05；词形 weigh。
- basket · n. · 篮子；义项 sense-staged-ox-1a7374e978b4a521-p06-c-18；来源 ox-1a7374e978b4a521；证据 p06-c-18-s05；词形 basket。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The centre is explicitly dry and uphill, so basement storage does not conflict with the flood context. The coordinator separates oversized tables, unchecked food boxes and blankets, resolves the scale capacity issue, and receives an accurate recap of locations and next work. The community logistics goal is concretely agreed.

## 缩短距离后继续练习击球 · p06-c-19

主题：文娱与运动；情景：调整初学板球击球练习（beginner-cricket-batting-practice）。

沟通目标：根据初学者遇到的困难改用更慢的近距离投球，并约定继续尝试

背景：奥利第一次参加板球体验，在多次没击中球后向教练法拉求助。

人物：Ollie、Coach Farah。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-19-s01 · Ollie: I have barely touched the ball all morning. Maybe I am just not athletic enough for this.
- p06-c-19-s02 · Coach Farah: You are learning a new movement. Let me see how you are holding the bat.
- p06-c-19-s03 · Ollie: Like this. Should I stand closer?
- p06-c-19-s04 · Coach Farah: Stay on that mark. I will move closer and send the ball more slowly so you have time to watch it.
- p06-c-19-s05 · Ollie: All right. I will have one more attempt before the break.
- p06-c-19-s06 · Coach Farah: Good. You do not have to abandon the whole activity because the first few tries were difficult.
- p06-c-19-s07 · Ollie: That slower ball was easier to follow. Could we do the same thing again?
- p06-c-19-s08 · Coach Farah: Yes. We will keep this distance for a few more tries.

目标用法：

- barely · adv. · 几乎不，勉强；义项 sense-staged-ox-b7f61db029c97218-p06-c-19；来源 ox-b7f61db029c97218；证据 p06-c-19-s01；词形 barely。
- athletic · adj. · 擅长运动的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c308d8be7b80605b-p06-c-19；来源 ox-c308d8be7b80605b；证据 p06-c-19-s01；词形 athletic。
- bat · n. · 球拍或击球用球棒；义项 sense-staged-ox-f73f34762eb0d7ca-p06-c-19；来源 ox-f73f34762eb0d7ca；证据 p06-c-19-s02；词形 bat。
- attempt · n. · 尝试；义项 sense-staged-ox-a3937944981eea29-p06-c-19；来源 ox-a3937944981eea29；证据 p06-c-19-s05；词形 attempt。
- abandon · v. · 放弃某项活动或打算；义项 sense-staged-ox-7feb8d51b7482c3f-p06-c-19；来源 ox-7feb8d51b7482c3f；证据 p06-c-19-s06；词形 abandon。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The novice expresses discouragement, the coach fixes his position and slows a closer delivery, and Ollie reports improved tracking before agreeing repeated practice. This is a coherent leisure coaching exchange with an observed result, not a promised successful hit; the title and revised-practice goal accurately match it.

## 商定父母周年聚餐的人数和时间 · p06-c-20

沟通目标：确定参加人数并为家庭纪念日协调见面时间。

背景：兄妹黛西和马尔科为父母结婚周年安排一顿小型家庭聚餐。

人物：Daisy、Marco。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-20-s01 · Daisy: Mum and Dad want their anniversary dinner to be a small affair, not a big party.
- p06-c-20-s02 · Marco: Then it is just them, us, and our partners. Six people altogether.
- p06-c-20-s03 · Daisy: Can everyone meet at the restaurant at seven on Saturday?
- p06-c-20-s04 · Marco: Yes. I checked with both partners, and Mum and Dad said seven suits them.
- p06-c-20-s05 · Daisy: Would a single gold balloon by their chairs be too much?
- p06-c-20-s06 · Marco: No, that would be nice. I will ask the restaurant if we can bring it when I reserve the table.
- p06-c-20-s07 · Daisy: And afterwards we can walk back to my flat for coffee, if they feel like it.
- p06-c-20-s08 · Marco: Good plan. I will request a table for six at seven, and you can bring the decoration once they confirm it is allowed.

The siblings correctly count parents, themselves and two partners as six, confirm everyone's Saturday-seven availability and allocate the reservation and conditional decoration. The relationships/family anniversary goal is coordination, not an already completed restaurant booking, and the final wording preserves that distinction. Mum, flat and afterwards are consistent regional choices.

## 在安全位置报告街头伤人事件 · p06-c-21

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：报告街头事件并等候救援（street-incident-emergency-call）。

沟通目标：向接线员说明伤人事件的位置和可见情况，在安全位置等候救援

背景：这是一段虚构的紧急联络语言练习：米娜在商店内目击门外有人受伤，与接线员沟通。

人物：Caller Mina、Operator Lee。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-21-s01 · Caller Mina: Someone has been attacked outside Green Street Library. Please send an ambulance.
- p06-c-21-s02 · Operator Lee: Are you in a safe place now?
- p06-c-21-s03 · Caller Mina: Yes, inside the shop opposite. The victim is sitting by the library steps. The other man was very aggressive and is still shouting down the road.
- p06-c-21-s04 · Operator Lee: Stay inside and do not approach him. Which entrance to the library can you see?
- p06-c-21-s05 · Caller Mina: The main entrance on Green Street, beside the bus stop. I can warn the shopkeeper not to let customers walk toward the shouting.
- p06-c-21-s06 · Operator Lee: Do that without leaving the shop. Help is being sent to the main library entrance. Stay on the line.
- p06-c-21-s07 · Caller Mina: I will stay here. The shopkeeper is keeping the door closed.

目标用法：

- ambulance · n. · 救护车；义项 sense-staged-ox-438d0594f3adb13d-p06-c-21；来源 ox-438d0594f3adb13d；证据 p06-c-21-s01；词形 ambulance。
- victim · n. · 受害者；义项 sense-staged-ox-156e5731ab71120f-p06-c-21；来源 ox-156e5731ab71120f；证据 p06-c-21-s03；词形 victim。
- aggressive · adj. · 咄咄逼人或有攻击性的；义项 sense-staged-ox-228d74b519848e3f-p06-c-21；来源 ox-228d74b519848e3f；证据 p06-c-21-s03；词形 aggressive。
- warn · v. · 提醒可能的危险，警告；义项 sense-staged-ox-b024f989b4701f06-p06-c-21；来源 ox-b024f989b4701f06；证据 p06-c-21-s05；词形 warn。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The caller reports an assault location, confirms she is inside a shop, distinguishes victim from the aggressive man and supplies the precise library entrance. The operator confirms help is being sent and the caller stays sheltered while the shopkeeper closes the door. This fictional community emergency-report scenario reaches its stated waiting-safely outcome without inventing an arrival of responders.

- ox-438d0594f3adb13d：accepted；s01 send an ambulance uses the count noun for the emergency medical vehicle requested for an injured person.（p06-c-21-s01）。
- ox-156e5731ab71120f：accepted；s03 The victim identifies the person harmed in the attack and contrasts that person with the shouting man, making the harm-recipient noun clear.（p06-c-21-s03）。
- ox-228d74b519848e3f：accepted；s03 was very aggressive uses the predicative adjective for threatening/hostile behaviour, reinforced by the continuing shouting.（p06-c-21-s03）。
- ox-b024f989b4701f06：accepted；s05 warn the shopkeeper not to let ... uses the verb with recipient and negative infinitive content, alerting someone to the nearby danger.（p06-c-21-s05）。

## 预约律师电话咨询上诉问题 · p06-c-22

背景：虚构服务对话中，安德烈致电律师事务所请求预约，不讨论应否上诉或法律结论。

人物：Andre、Receptionist Hazel。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-22-s01 · Andre: I need to speak to an attorney about an appeal. Can I arrange a telephone appointment?
- p06-c-22-s02 · Receptionist Hazel: Certainly. We have Tuesday at ten thirty or Wednesday at two.
- p06-c-22-s03 · Andre: Tuesday at ten thirty would work. What should I have with me?
- p06-c-22-s04 · Receptionist Hazel: Have the decision letter ready so the attorney can understand what you are asking about. I cannot advise you on the appeal myself.
- p06-c-22-s05 · Andre: Understood. Please book Tuesday under Andre Bell.
- p06-c-22-s06 · Receptionist Hazel: Your telephone appointment is booked for Tuesday at ten thirty. I will send the confirmation and the office contact details.

目标用法：

- attorney · n. · 律师；义项 sense-staged-ox-3b7b3edbab4efe5a-p06-c-22；来源 ox-3b7b3edbab4efe5a；证据 p06-c-22-s01；词形 attorney。
- appeal · n. · 请求重新审理或改变裁定的上诉；义项 sense-staged-ox-046288e092f45249-p06-c-22；来源 ox-046288e092f45249；证据 p06-c-22-s01；词形 appeal。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The caller requests a professional consultation about an appeal, selects one of two slots, asks what to have ready and explicitly books under his name. The receptionist confirms Tuesday at ten thirty while reserving legal advice to the attorney. The services appointment goal is achieved, not a legal merits decision; attorney is consistent with an otherwise region-neutral exchange.

- ox-046288e092f45249：accepted；s01 about an appeal uses the article-marked noun for a challenge to a decision, clarified by the decision letter in s04, not the verb or attractiveness sense.（p06-c-22-s01）。

## 移开纸张并吸干桌上清水 · p06-c-23

主题：学习与技能；情景：清理课堂桌面泼水（classroom-water-spill-cleanup）。

沟通目标：把学习材料移离积水并分工吸干桌面后继续课堂活动

背景：罗莎在手工课桌面打翻一杯清水，桌上没有电器，老师协助她保护纸模型并清理。

人物：Student Rosa、Teacher Chen。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-23-s01 · Student Rosa: My desk is an absolute mess. I have knocked water over the paper models.
- p06-c-23-s02 · Teacher Chen: Put the dry models aside on this tray first. I will lift the wet sheet off the table.
- p06-c-23-s03 · Student Rosa: Shall I wipe it up with some paper?
- p06-c-23-s04 · Teacher Chen: Use this cloth to absorb the puddle instead. Then we can see which sheets need replacing.
- p06-c-23-s05 · Student Rosa: The table is dry now, and these three models were not touched. I only need one new sheet.
- p06-c-23-s06 · Teacher Chen: Here you are. Keep the cup on the separate shelf while you finish.

目标用法：

- absolute · adj. · 完全的，十足的；用于加强语气；义项 sense-staged-ox-3d69bee6fb43fa77-p06-c-23；来源 ox-3d69bee6fb43fa77；证据 p06-c-23-s01；词形 absolute。
- aside · adv. · 到一旁；义项 sense-staged-ox-30c2f786c9c5f945-p06-c-23；来源 ox-30c2f786c9c5f945；证据 p06-c-23-s02；词形 aside。
- absorb · v. · 吸收液体；义项 sense-staged-ox-127a24710464c2c0-p06-c-23；来源 ox-127a24710464c2c0；证据 p06-c-23-s04；词形 absorb。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The teacher first protects dry models, then supplies a cloth, and the student confirms a dry table and a single replacement sheet. The final separate-cup location permits resuming the craft activity, matching the learning cleanup goal. Absorb the puddle is understandable physical-liquid usage, although absorb the water would be a slightly more usual wording; it is not a semantic error.

绑定文件 SHA-256：e2b56d2d8cd55c57fbf02c4dda61f3b5aa681b13ec0b6da7409edbe89551e120

- ox-3d69bee6fb43fa77：accepted；s01 an absolute mess is the common emphatic adjective-noun combination meaning a complete mess, not a claim of philosophical absoluteness.（p06-c-23-s01）。
- ox-30c2f786c9c5f945：accepted；s02 put the dry models aside uses the directional adverb without its own object; on this tray separately specifies where the models go.（p06-c-23-s02）。
- ox-127a24710464c2c0：accepted；s04 cloth to absorb the puddle uses the transitive verb for taking up liquid into material. Puddle denotes the spilled water here; the dry-table result confirms the physical absorption sense.（p06-c-23-s04）。

## 按邮件附件启用线上展览访问 · p06-c-24

主题：消费与服务；情景：解决线上展览访问问题（online-exhibition-access-help）。

沟通目标：找到邮件说明中的访问启用链接并确认展览可以打开

背景：埃米尔联系虚构线上画廊的支持人员，询问全球开放的展览为何仍显示欢迎页面。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-24-s01 · Visitor Emil: Your email announced a worldwide launch today, but I still cannot enter the exhibition.
- p06-c-24-s02 · Support Jules: Does the page show a welcome message or an error?
- p06-c-24-s03 · Visitor Emil: A welcome message. I clicked the main picture in the email.
- p06-c-24-s04 · Support Jules: The instructions are in the PDF attachment. Open that and use the link labelled Activate my visit.
- p06-c-24-s05 · Visitor Emil: Do I have to activate it every time?
- p06-c-24-s06 · Support Jules: No, just for this first visit. After that, save the gallery address.
- p06-c-24-s07 · Visitor Emil: I have opened the link, and now I can see the first room. That was the step I missed.
- p06-c-24-s08 · Support Jules: Great. You can move between rooms using the arrows at the bottom.

- attachment · n. · 电子邮件附件；义项 sense-staged-ox-d628325ed7371b17-p06-c-24；来源 ox-d628325ed7371b17；证据 p06-c-24-s04；词形 attachment。
- activate · v. · 使开始运行或生效，启用；义项 sense-staged-ox-888f428101561fcd-p06-c-24；来源 ox-888f428101561fcd；证据 p06-c-24-s05；词形 activate。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Support distinguishes a welcome screen from an error, identifies the missed first-visit link in the email attachment and explains that activation is one-time. Emil actually opens the first room and confirms success, fulfilling the services access-resolution goal. The fictional site design is somewhat unusual but internally consistent, with a clear difference between the email image and activation link.

- ox-d628325ed7371b17：accepted；s04 PDF attachment is the noun for an email-enclosed file, anchored to the email already discussed in s01 and s03.（p06-c-24-s04）。

## 确认农场参观的宠物和围栏规则 · p06-c-25

主题：自然与环境；情景：核对农场参观规则（farm-visit-animal-rules）。

沟通目标：确认宠物限制并了解观赏牲畜的位置

背景：学习农业的努尔到教学农场咨询周末参观安排，导览员彼得说明参观范围和动物区规则。

人物：Visitor Noor、Guide Peter。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-25-s01 · Visitor Noor: I am studying agriculture, and I would like to see your crop trial on Saturday.
- p06-c-25-s02 · Guide Peter: Of course. That field covers one acre, and the visitor path goes around its edge.
- p06-c-25-s03 · Visitor Noor: Can I bring my dog if I keep her on a lead?
- p06-c-25-s04 · Guide Peter: I am afraid pets cannot enter the farm. The animal area is part of the same visitor route.
- p06-c-25-s05 · Visitor Noor: I will leave her at home. Where can visitors see the goats?
- p06-c-25-s06 · Guide Peter: From the path behind the wooden barrier. Please stay on the visitor side and do not feed them.
- p06-c-25-s07 · Visitor Noor: Thanks. I will follow the marked path around the field and then look at the goats from there.

目标用法：

- agriculture · n. · 农业；义项 sense-staged-ox-cf4260f00cebaf37-p06-c-25；来源 ox-cf4260f00cebaf37；证据 p06-c-25-s01；词形 agriculture。
- acre · n. · 英亩，土地面积单位；义项 sense-staged-ox-49b738c0fb4aa45a-p06-c-25；来源 ox-49b738c0fb4aa45a；证据 p06-c-25-s02；词形 acre。
- barrier · n. · 阻挡或分隔区域的围栏或障碍物；义项 sense-staged-ox-9ca5580648dc2259-p06-c-25；来源 ox-9ca5580648dc2259；证据 p06-c-25-s06；词形 barrier。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The visitor identifies a crop-trial interest, receives the field route, learns pets are excluded, agrees to leave the dog home and repeats the safe viewing plan behind the barrier. The nature/farm-visit category and pet/boundary goal align with the actual exchange; the British on a lead is natural in this context.

- ox-49b738c0fb4aa45a：accepted；s02 covers one acre names a land-area unit after the numeral one, an unambiguous singular noun matching the B2 source candidate.（p06-c-25-s02）。

- p06-c-26-s03 · Hugo: Yes, please. You can drop me at the corner; there is no need to turn into the narrow lane.

## 分配剩菜避免浪费 · p06-c-27

主题：饮食沟通；情景：安排共享剩菜晚餐（shared-leftovers-plan）。

沟通目标：根据现有食物和个人偏好分配晚餐并商定处理多余食物

背景：合住者莉娜和奥马尔查看冰箱里今晚可以吃的剩菜。

人物：Lena、Omar。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-27-s01 · Lena: There are two portions of vegetable stew and one of pasta. I do not want to waste any of it.
- p06-c-27-s02 · Omar: I would prefer the pasta. You can have whatever is in the blue container if you like it.
- p06-c-27-s03 · Lena: That is the stew, and it is plenty for me. What should we do with the extra portion?
- p06-c-27-s04 · Omar: Freeze it tonight for another meal. Basically, we each eat one portion now and save the third.
- p06-c-27-s05 · Lena: Good. I will label the spare portion before putting it in the freezer, and then we can heat our dinner.

目标用法：

- waste · v. · 浪费，使有用的东西未得到使用；义项 sense-staged-ox-23a29629227e461c-p06-c-27；来源 ox-23a29629227e461c；证据 p06-c-27-s01；词形 waste。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The housemates allocate the one pasta portion and one stew portion, then agree to label and freeze the second stew portion. Three portions are accounted for without contradiction, and the food/leftover-use goal ends with an actionable division of work rather than merely an anti-waste slogan.

## 先评估社团设备再编维修预算 · p06-c-28

主题：工作与协作；情景：安排年度设备审查（club-annual-equipment-review）。

沟通目标：核对设备清单与状况评估的区别，并安排检查后再确定维修预算

背景：小型表演社团的财务负责人贝森和管理员艾萨克准备年度设备记录。

人物：Treasurer Bethan、Manager Isaac。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-28-s01 · Treasurer Bethan: Our accountant needs the equipment list by Friday. Do we check every item annually?
- p06-c-28-s02 · Manager Isaac: Yes. I have counted the chairs and lights, but the piano still needs a condition assessment.
- p06-c-28-s03 · Treasurer Bethan: It is the club's most valuable asset. A price on a list will not tell us whether it needs work.
- p06-c-28-s04 · Manager Isaac: Exactly. Do you anticipate any major repairs this year?
- p06-c-28-s05 · Treasurer Bethan: I cannot say yet. One key sticks, but we should get the technician's report before putting a repair figure in the budget.
- p06-c-28-s06 · Manager Isaac: I will arrange that inspection and mark the condition as not yet assessed. You can send the counted items to the accountant now.
- p06-c-28-s07 · Treasurer Bethan: Agreed. I will explain that the piano report will follow, rather than inventing a cost.

目标用法：

- accountant · n. · 会计师，会计人员；义项 sense-staged-ox-d42c0ffdc102af1f-p06-c-28；来源 ox-d42c0ffdc102af1f；证据 p06-c-28-s01；词形 accountant。
- annually · adv. · 每年一次地；义项 sense-staged-ox-2f53d2c4c45778ad-p06-c-28；来源 ox-2f53d2c4c45778ad；证据 p06-c-28-s01；词形 annually。
- assessment · n. · 评估；义项 sense-staged-ox-f2c78bdcf32b132a-p06-c-28；来源 ox-f2c78bdcf32b132a；证据 p06-c-28-s02；词形 assessment。
- asset · n. · 有价值的财产或资产；义项 sense-staged-ox-f96739355bcd42e2-p06-c-28；来源 ox-f96739355bcd42e2；证据 p06-c-28-s03；词形 asset。
- anticipate · v. · 预料，预计；义项 sense-staged-ox-b4558156f76d230e-p06-c-28；来源 ox-b4558156f76d230e；证据 p06-c-28-s04；词形 anticipate。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The treasurer and manager distinguish a counted equipment inventory from a technician's condition judgement and refuse to fabricate a repair estimate. Isaac takes responsibility for inspection while Bethan sends completed counts with the piano caveat. The work/equipment-review goal is an agreed evidence-first budget process, not an unperformed final assessment.

## 查明申请页面为何仍显示待补材料 · p06-c-29

主题：学习与技能；情景：询问课程申请材料状态（course-application-status-inquiry）。

沟通目标：核对申请材料是否收到，澄清页面更新延迟与录取决定的区别

背景：泰莎已上传课程申请附件，但界面仍显示缺少材料，她向课程行政人员马利克查询。

人物：Applicant Tessa、Administrator Malik。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-29-s01 · Applicant Tessa: I am an applicant for the evening design course. Apparently my portfolio is missing, but I uploaded it yesterday.
- p06-c-29-s02 · Administrator Malik: Let me check your record. What is your application number?
- p06-c-29-s03 · Applicant Tessa: D forty-two. I also have an email confirming the upload.
- p06-c-29-s04 · Administrator Malik: I can see the file now. The status page has not updated yet; your materials are complete.
- p06-c-29-s05 · Applicant Tessa: Does that mean I have a place on the course?
- p06-c-29-s06 · Administrator Malik: Not yet. The tutors still have to review the applications. You do not need to upload the file again.
- p06-c-29-s07 · Applicant Tessa: Thanks for checking. I will keep the receipt and wait for the review email.

- apparently · adv. · 根据所见或所得信息似乎如此；义项 sense-staged-ox-3018c3979693deeb-p06-c-29；来源 ox-3018c3979693deeb；证据 p06-c-29-s01；词形 apparently。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Tessa provides her application reference and upload receipt, Malik confirms the file is present and the page stale, then explicitly separates material completeness from admission. Tessa keeps the receipt and awaits review instead of duplicating the upload. The learning/application-status goal and title are precisely resolved.

## 确认成人零基础也能参加芭蕾体验课 · p06-c-30

主题：文娱与运动；情景：报名舞蹈社团体验课（beginner-dance-enrolment）。

沟通目标：确认零基础参加者可以独自参加并约好首次到场时间

背景：任在社区舞蹈社团咨询成人初学者体验课，西莉娅是该班教师。

人物：Visitor Ren、Instructor Celia。

起草：author-phase06-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p06-c-30-s01 · Visitor Ren: I always associate ballet with children who started very young. Is this class really for adult beginners?
- p06-c-30-s02 · Instructor Celia: Yes. No previous dance experience is needed; we start with the simplest movements.
- p06-c-30-s03 · Visitor Ren: Can I come on my own? None of my friends wants to try it.
- p06-c-30-s04 · Instructor Celia: Absolutely. You do not need a partner. The next taster session is Friday at six.
- p06-c-30-s05 · Visitor Ren: Then I would like to join that session. What should I bring?
- p06-c-30-s06 · Instructor Celia: Comfortable clothes and socks are enough for the first visit. Come to the main studio ten minutes early.
- p06-c-30-s07 · Visitor Ren: Great. I will be there at five fifty on Friday.

目标用法：

- associate · v. · 把某事物与另一事物联系起来；义项 sense-staged-ox-2d379f4090ef6911-p06-c-30；来源 ox-2d379f4090ef6911；证据 p06-c-30-s01；词形 associate。
- ballet · n. · 芭蕾舞；义项 sense-staged-ox-51fa47580890b37d-p06-c-30；来源 ox-51fa47580890b37d；证据 p06-c-30-s01；词形 ballet。

审阅者：reviewer-phase06-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The instructor directly resolves the adult beginner and solo-attendance concerns, offers the Friday-six taster and gives minimal preparation and an early-arrival instruction. Ren agrees to arrive at 5:50, correctly applying the ten-minute lead time. This fulfils the leisure trial-class arrangement without implying prior dance experience or a partner is required.
